How to use this knowledge base when answering questions about Hypixel SkyBlock Wiki:
- Answer from this content only. If it is not covered, say so; never guess numbers.
- Find relevant pages in index.md by title, aliases (aka) and categories.
- [[X]] links to page X; [[X|y]] and 'y ([[X]])' are text y linking to X. Follow links and each page's 'Linked from' list to gather related facts (e.g. where an item drops or is used).
- Cite the [[Page]] each fact comes from. Reply in the user's language.

# Happy Mask
Aliases: Dante Mask
Categories: Legacy items, Uncommon items, Armor, The Uprising, Items, Helmet
Legacy Item This item is no longer obtainable other than trading with players, except in the [[Stranded|☀ Stranded]] Gamemode.
[Happy Mask]
Type: [[Armor]]
Rarity: UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
Tooltip Text: Wear this mask to show your support for [[Dante]].
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Merchants: [[Dante]]
Buy: 100 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: HAPPY_MASK
Tooltip: Wear this mask to show your support for Dante.; This item can be reforged!; UNCOMMON HELMET
The Happy Mask is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] helmet which could be purchased from [[Dante]] for 100 [[Coins]]. It allowed the player to access [[Official Buildings]] when Dante was the elected [[Mayor Election|mayor]].
## Usage
Players could wear the mask to be granted access to the [[Official Buildings]], unless they were carrying a [[Dante's Talisman]]. Otherwise, the [[Goons]] would not let them in.
## Trivia
- This item does the same thing as the [[Dante's Talisman]].
- The custom head seems to be modeled after Dante's face.
- The mask may be a reference to the game We Happy Few, as the game features a mask with a similar name and purpose.
- This item followed the rules of every other NPC item as you could only buy 640 a day.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Apr 4, 2021|Added Happy Mask.|
|Apr 16, 2021|The Happy Mask is no longer obtainable|

# Jacob's Participation Medal
Categories: Legacy items, Epic items, Items, Craftable items, Confirmations needed, Minion Modifiers
Legacy Item This item is no longer obtainable other than trading with players.
[Jacob's Participation Medal]
Type: Item
Rarity: EPIC ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 32x [[Botroot]] 1x [[Jacob's Ticket]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: JACOBS_PARTICIPATION_MEDAL
Tooltip: Minion Fuel Legacy Item; Use this as Fuel on ONE Farming minion, and it will occasionally generate Jacob's Tickets for 3d.; A recreation of a medal Jacob used to give out for his contests. They are too popular now to be given out.; EPIC
Jacob's Participation Medal is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Minion Fuel]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
Jacob's Participation Medal is no longer craftable. The recipe used to be the following:
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|1x [[Jacob's Ticket]] 32x [[Botroot]]|Recipe: [[Botroot]] x8 ×4 + [[Jacob's Ticket]] → Jacob's Participation Medal|
## Usage
When placed in the fuel slot of a [[Farming]] [[Minions|Minion]], Jacob's Participation Medal generates 1 [[Jacob's Ticket]] every 3 hours. The fuel lasts for 3 days. Only one minion can have Jacob's Participation Medals at a time.
## Bugs
- When a Jacob's Participation Medal runs out of time while the private island is unloaded, no [[Jacob's Ticket|Jacob's Tickets]] will be made for the entire time the island has been unloaded.[Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)]
## Trivia
- A Jacob's Participation Medal generates across its duration, for a total profit of . Because it costs so much more than it makes across its duration, Jacob's Participation Medal got replaced with [[Super Jacob Entertainment System]].
## See Also
- [[Super Jacob Entertainment System]]
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Mar 31, 2026|Added Jacob's Participation Medal.|
|Jul 15, 2026|Made Jacob's Participation Medal a legacy item.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Apr 28, 2026|0.24.4|Added Jacob's Participation Medal.|
|Jul 22, 2026|0.26.1|Made Jacob's Participation Medal a legacy item.|
Linked from: [[Collectable Items]], [[Super Jacob Entertainment System]]

## Obtaining
### Crafting
The Jerry Helmet can be crafted using 5 [[Move Jerry]] while the [[A Honk and Seek Adventure]] event is active. This is a secret recipe that does not appear in Quick Craft or the Recipe Book.
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|

## Usage
Helmet:

[Effects]
[Ability: Think; Channel your inner Jerry.]
### [[An Extremely Difficult Item For You To Find]]
During [[A Honk and Seek Adventure]], the Jerry Helmet is used to find [[An Extremely Difficult Item For You To Find]].
## Trivia
- Jerry Helmet was still craftable for months after [[A Honk and Seek Adventure]] ended, however, it has since been patched and is no longer craftable.

# Lost Bottle of Jyrre
Aliases: Lost Bottle Of Jyrre
Categories: Legacy items, Article stubs, Common items, Pages with unknown currency, Items, Events, Season of Jerry, Consumables, V0.7.5, Pages with math equations
Legacy Item This item is no longer obtainable other than trading with players.
This article needs more information. Please help expand it.
See more stubs.
[Lost Bottle of Jyrre]
Type: Item
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: Unknown currency 100 coins + 100 coins per 20 Minutes + 100 coins per 20 Minutes per 20 Levels of Intelligence coins
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: BOTTLE_OF_JYRRE
Tooltip: Provides a boost to your  Intelligence! The longer this item stays in your inventory, the more intelligent you will be after drinking it. Must be kept in your inventory at all times!; Intelligence Bonus: 0; Duration: 10 Minutes; COMMON
Lost Bottle of Jyrre is an unobtainable [[Rarity|COMMON]] item. It used to be purchased from [[Sherry]] for 50,000 [[Coins]].
It is a legacy item, replaced with a new [[Bottle of Jyrre]].
## Usage
Upon drinking, it will provide a temporary boost to [[Intelligence]]. The amount of intelligence granted is based on the amount of time the bottle has been in the player's inventory; however, the intelligence bonus is currently capped at 100. The effect wears off after 10 minutes.
If the bottle is moved to any place besides the inventory, the intelligence bonus on the potion will be reset. The space considered the inventory does not include the [[Potion Bag]] or the [[Ender Chest]]. The bottle does not reset when traded on the [[Auction House|Auction]].
Players can use only one potion at a time, as trying to use another gives them the message "You already have an active Bottle of Jyrre!"
## Intelligence & Price
To accumulate X intelligence, it needs
20X+X(X+1)2 minutes.
Additionally, to accumulate 1 more level of intelligence when the current intelligence is X, the bottle requires
20 + X minutes.
So to get from 1 to 2 intelligence is: (20*2+(2*3)/2)−(20*1+(1*2)/2)=43−21=22 min.
And to get from 40 to 41 intelligence, the bottle requires: 40 + 20 = 60 minutes.
Price starts at 100 coins and increases every 20 minutes by 100 coins. Additionally, every 20 levels of intelligence the increase of value increases by 100 coins.
|Int ([[Intelligence]])|[[Coins]]/Lvl|whole "zone"|
|-|-|-|
|1-20|+100|2000|
|20-40|+200|4000|
|40-60|+300|6000|
|60-80|+400|8000|
|80-100|+500|10k|
Accumulated intelligence does not have a cap.
|Intelligence|Time to +1 (upgrade)|Cumulative||mini. Value|Time to get whole bottle|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|0|0|0||100||
|1|1,200|1,200|20 minutes|200|20 min|
|2|1,260|2,460|21 minutes|300|41 min|
|3|1,320|3,780|22 minutes|400|63 min|
|...|..|..||||
|20|2,400|35,400|39 minutes|2,100|9h 50m|
|21|2,460|37,800|40 minutes|2,300|10h 30m|
|...|...|...||||
|40|3,600|94,800|59 minutes|6,100|26h 20m|
|41|3,660|98,400|60 minutes|6,400|27h 20m|
|...|...|...||||
|60|4,800|178,200|79 minutes|12,100|49h 30m|
|61|4,860|183,000|80 minutes|12,500|50h 50m|
|...|...|...||||
|...|...|...||||
|254|16,380|2,232,660|273 minutes|164,200|620h 11m 25d 20h 11m|

If the calculator fails to load up, please report to an Administrator.
## Trivia
- Jyrre is also an anagram of Jerry. It is likely that the developers did this on purpose.
- In a brewing stand, a Bottle of Jyrre acts much like a bottle of water, as it can be turned into an awkward potion using [[Nether Wart|nether warts]], which is not recommended.
- The NPC sell price of a Bottle of Jyrre will continue to increase even after reaching its maximum value.

|Jul 30, 2020|Bottle of Jyrre [[Intelligence]] boost capped at 100.|
|Nov 30, 2022|Bottle of Jyrre no longer obtainable from [[Gifts]].|

|Nov 28, 2023|0.19.8|Lost Bottle of Jyrre can no longer be obtained.|

Magma Bow is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Bows|Bow]] that was previously craftable, with its recipe unlocked at [[Magma Cream|Magma Cream IX (9)]]. Its ability consumes 1 [[Magma Cream]] to deal double damage.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
The Magma Bow can no longer be crafted. When it could, it had the following recipe:
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Magma Cream IX (9) ([[Magma Cream]])|3x [[Enchanted String]] 96x (1 stack plus 32 (96 in total)) [[Enchanted Magma Cream]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Magma Cream]] x32 ×3 + [[Enchanted String]] ×3 → Magma Bow|
## Usage
Bow:
Magma Bow [[Damage]]: +100 [[Strength]]: +100
[Effects]
[Ability: Arrow Infusion ON SHOOT; Consumes 1 Magma Cream from the Inventory (Player) or Quiver to double the damage per shot.]
## Trivia
- The Ability consumes both an [[Arrows|Arrow]] and Magma Cream.
- [[Infinite Quiver]] saves Magma Cream when used on this Bow.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Feb 22, 2022|Removed Magma Bow recipe.|
|Jun 24, 2026|Added a custom texture for Magma Bow as part of the SkyBlock Resource Pack.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|Added Magma Bow.|
|Aug 19, 2020|Magma Bow x2 damage now applies post-multiplier, such that shots using Magma Cream now truly deal 2x the damage of shots without Magma Cream.|

Tooltip: An ancient blueprint.; This item can be reforged!; COMMON FARMING TOOL
The Mathematical Hoe Blueprint was a [[Rarity|COMMON]] item which can be crafted into 5 specialized [[Hoes]].
## Obtaining
A Mathematical Hoe Blueprint could be purchased from [[Anita]] for 1 [[Medals|Gold Medal]] and 32 [[Jacob's Ticket|Jacob's Tickets]].
## Usage
Mathematical Hoe Blueprints used to be able to be crafted into the following hoes:
- [[Euclid's Wheat Sickle|Euclid's Wheat Hoe]]
- [[Gauss Carrot Shovel|Gauss Carrot Hoe]]
- [[Pythagorean Potato Shovel|Pythagorean Potato Hoe]]
- [[Turing Sugar Cane Cutter|Turing Sugar Cane Hoe]]
- [[Newton Nether Wart Cutter|Newton Nether Warts Hoe]]
## Trivia
- There was an auction bug where [[Rarity|RARE]] Mathematical Hoes with rarity upgraded by reaching 100,000 counter cannot have their BIN price raised above 5x their NPC sell value.
- You could apply enchantments to a Mathematical Hoe Blueprint and they would be preserved when crafted into a specialized hoe.

# Phantom Rod
Categories: Legacy items, Legendary items, Gemstone upgradeable items, Fishing Rods, Tools, Fishing, Weapons, Mob Drops, Fishing Weapons

[Phantom Rod]
Type: Fishing Weapon

Obtained: [[Phantom Fisher]] drop
Skill Level ([[Skills]]) Requirement: Fishing XXI (21) ([[Fishing]])
Stats: [[Damage]]: +200; [[Strength]]: +125; [[Fishing Speed]]: +100
Gemstones: ⚔ Combat Gemstone Slot (Applicable Gemstones Ruby Amethyst Sapphire Jasper Onyx Opal; Cost to unlock 50,000 coins 20 Fine Jasper 20 Fine Sapphire 20 Fine Ruby 20 Fine Amethyst) ⚔ Combat Gemstone Slot (Applicable Gemstones Ruby Amethyst Sapphire Jasper Onyx Opal; Cost to unlock 200,000 coins 40 Fine Jasper 40 Fine Sapphire 40 Fine Ruby 40 Fine Amethyst)
[Item Properties]
Enchantable: ✔ Yes
Reforgeable: ✔ Yes
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes

Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✔ Yes (+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]]))
[Shop]
Sell: 1,000 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: PHANTOM_ROD
Tooltip: Damage: +200; Strength: +125; Fishing Speed: +100; This rod is broken and cannot be used to fish.; Bring it to Roddy in the Backwater Bayou to convert it into a new rod!; This item can be reforged!; ❣ Requires Fishing Skill 21.; LEGENDARY FISHING ROD
A Phantom Rod was a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Fishing Rods|Fishing Weapon]] dropped from the [[Phantom Fisher]]. It required [[Fishing|Fishing XXI (21)]] to use.
## Usage
Fishing Rod:
Phantom Rod [[Damage]]: +200 [[Strength]]: +125 [[Fishing Speed]]: +100
[Effects]
[Ability: Phantom Impel LEFT CLICK; Terrify hooked enemies pushing them away and dealing massive damage.]
The Phantom Rod could be used as a fishing rod and weapon. Both fishing and sword [[Enchantments]] could be applied to it.
The Phantom Rod's ability terrified mobs hit by the hook, dealing [[Damage Calculation|Melee Damage]] and knocking them back. This had no cooldown beyond [[Attack Speed]].
## Essence Crafting
This item could be upgraded with [[Undead Essence]]. It had no other requirements when converted.
Essence Required; [[Undead Essence]]:
|Action|Cost|Cumul. (Cumulative)|
|-|-|-|
|Conversion (The cost of the conversion to a dungeon item.)|500|500|
|✪|200|700|
|✪✪|300|1,000|
|✪✪✪|400|1,400|
|✪✪✪✪|500; 10,000 [[Coins]]|1,900; 10,000 [[Coins]]|
|✪✪✪✪✪|600; 25,000 [[Coins]]|2,500; 35,000 [[Coins]]|
## Trivia
- When the Phantom Rod was being removed in the version 0.22 Alpha, the Hypixel development team stated under the second day's changes that there are plans to add a new [[Rod Parts|Sinker]] which applies the Phantom Rod's ability to any fishing rod. The Sinker with Phantom Rod's abilities has not currently released yet, as the team said it might take a while as it might necessitate nerfs to either the Phantom Impel ability or the [[Shredded Line]] in order to make them both compatible and balanced.
- Although the weapon is removed and no longer usable even for players who already have it, the Phantom Rod is still used by the [[Phantom Fisher]], which uses the old vanilla fishing rod texture rather than the current broken fishing rod texture.
- This was one of two fishing weapons in the game, the other being the [[Shredder]].
- This weapon could stunlock [[Thorn]], similar to the [[Bonemerang]].
- Endermen were immune to any attacks from the Phantom Rod.
- The Phantom Rod was the only item in the game that could be dungeonized to increase [[Sea Creature Chance]].
## Tips
- The Phantom Rod was a very powerful weapon against dungeon mini-bosses such as [[Lost Adventurer|Lost Adventurers]] and [[Angry Archaeologist|Angry Archaeologists]], but also gained time to finish the Terminals in [[The Catacombs - Floor VII]], [[Goldor]] Fight. Once hooked, they could be locked in place by left clicking and have significant damage done to them.
  - The rod could be used to easily kill [[The Watcher|Watcher]] summons from afar, allowing the player to avoid their attacks.
- This weapon paired well with low [[Effective Health]] dungeon builds, as it would turn melee classes into ranged classes. It could be a low-budget option for [[Berserk|Berserkers]], and could completely negate the use of a [[Baby Yeti Pet]].
- This weapon was a great weapon to deal damage to [[Arachne]]. By hooking her with the Phantom Rod, and spamming left click, damage could be dealt through the cobwebs. This also made a player a lot safer, as the player could keep distance from her while dealing damage.
  - The Phantom Rod did not work nearly as well on the [[Tarantula Broodfather]] due to the boss constantly slam-dunking into the player. Additionally, it prevented it from dealing damage to the player frequently enough, so that the boss rapidly regenerated [[Health]].
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Mar 12, 2025|Removed Phantom Rod.|
|Mar 14, 2025|Announced plans for a Sinker ([[Rod Parts]]) with Phantom Rod's old ability which may "take a while".|
|Jun 24, 2026|Added a custom texture for Phantom Rod as part of the SkyBlock Resource Pack.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Nov 2, 2020|0.9.10|Added Phantom Rod.|
|Aug 18, 2022|0.14|Now gives +100 [[Fishing Speed]].|
|Sep 17, 2024|0.20.6|Added 2x Combat Gemstone Slots ([[Gemstone Slot]]) to the Phantom Rod.|
|Mar 18, 2025|0.22|Removed Phantom Rod.|
|Jul 8, 2026|0.26|Added a custom texture for Phantom Rod as part of the SkyBlock Resource Pack.|

# Pig Mask

Type: [[Armor]]

[Item Properties]
Enchantable: ✔ Yes

Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: PIG_MASK
Tooltip: Speed: +1; Wear this mask to show your support for the Dante resistance.; Cosmetic item!; This item can be reforged!; LEGENDARY HELMET
The Pig Mask is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Helmets|Helmet]] which can be bought from the [[Piggy|Technoshop]]. It's a cosmetic item made to celebrate the [[Resistance Movement]]'s victory over [[Dante]]. It can only be bought once per profile, which makes it like the [[Potato Talisman]], etc.
## Obtaining
The Pig Mask could be purchased from the [[Piggy|Technoshop]] for 2 [[Enchanted Raw Porkchop]] and 10,000 [[Coins]] while [[Resistance Leader|Technoblade]] was mayor. Only one could be obtained per profile.
On [[Stranded]] only (This content is only available on the Stranded game mode.), multiple Pig Masks can be purchased from [[Stranded|Villagers]] for 50 [[Emerald|Emeralds]] each.
## Trivia
- The Pig Mask's design and +1 [[Speed]] are references to YouTuber Technoblade and his liking for the stat.
  - The fact that it only grants +1 [[Speed]] may reference his frustration with all the [[Fairy Souls]] (as of version 0.1) only giving him a base +3 [[Speed]], an amount that isn't great enough to surpass sprint-jumping at normal speed.
- This item was added as part of YouTuber Technoblade's mayor perks, due to his position as a [[Resistance Leader]].
- This seems to be the Resistance counterpart of the [[Happy Mask]].
  - Technoblade has stated in one of his Streams that he thinks the Happy Mask is actually a reasonable demand due to social distancing which may indicate why the Pig Mask is a replacement for it.
## History

# Pioneer Pickaxe
Aliases: Alpha Pick, Alpha Pickaxe
Categories: Legacy items, Special items, Items, Tools, Mining, Hypixel Gifts
Legacy Item This item is no longer obtainable other than trading with players.
[Pioneer Pickaxe]
Type: Pickaxe ([[Pickaxes]])
Rarity: SPECIAL ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Enchantable: ✔ Yes
Reforgeable: ✔ Yes
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✔ Yes
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: ALPHA_PICK
Tooltip: Breaking Power 1; Mining Speed: +1; The very first pickaxe model!; Invented by the famous Thomas Pickson.; This item can be reforged!; SPECIAL PICKAXE
The Pioneer Pickaxe is a [[Rarity|SPECIAL]] item, awarded to players who participated in the testing of the [[Crystal Hollows]] on the [[Alpha Hypixel Network]].
## Obtaining
A Pioneer Pickaxe was obtainable by logging on to the [[Alpha Hypixel Network|Alpha Network]] at least once during testing for the [[Crystal Hollows]]. It could be claimed from [[Jerry]]'s Personal Deliveries if the player met said requirements.
## Usage
Pickaxe:
Pioneer Pickaxe [[Breaking Power]]: +1 [[Mining Speed]]: +1
## Trivia
- The inventor of this item, Thomas Pickson, is a reference to Thomas Edison, often called America's greatest inventor.
- The Pioneer Pickaxe can only be reforged with [[Reforge Stones]] and not [[Reforging|basic reforging]].
- Because the pickaxe is [[Rarity|SPECIAL]], when reforged with [[Reforging|Refined]], it gives better stats than the [[Stonk]], making it give more [[Skills|Skill]] XP when collecting Mining [[Minions]].
  - When [[Reforging|Reforged]] to [[Reforging|Refined]] and [[Recombobulator 3000|Recombobulated]], this item gives 9 [[Mining Wisdom]] when held. This can be increased to 19 [[Mining Wisdom]] with [[Compact|Compact X]]. This is the highest mining exp boost possible from any held item. This pickaxe would also give a 0.7% chance to grant you the enchanted version of blocks you mine (because of Compact).
- This item does 0.25x as much damage as the vanilla [[Wooden Pickaxe]], suggesting the item has 3.75 [[Damage]]. However, to enemies that aren't [[Glacite Walker|Glacite Walkers]], it does the same damage as one of the player's fists, and to Glacite Walkers it does 1000x higher damage than fists, contrary to the usual ~154x.
- Pioneer Pickaxe is the only non-[[Mementos|Memento]] item given to players for [[Alpha Hypixel Network|Alpha]] testing.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jul 20, 2021|Added Pioneer Pickaxe.|
|Unknown Date|Removed extra line in lore.|

- The item's quote is a reference to Technoblade's videos about the Potato War, in which he quoted Sun Tzu's The Art of War.
- The Potato Crown granting +1 [[Speed]] could be a reference to Technoblade's video about him being the fastest man on SkyBlock. In the video, the reaction from the stat bonus the [[Bat Talisman]] gave could be why it gives +1 [[Speed]].

A Ragna Rock was a [[Rarity|SPECIAL]] item obtained during [[A Honk and Seek Adventure]].
## Obtaining
A Ragna Rock had a 4% (1 in 25) chance to drop from any [[Crimson Isle]] miniboss during [[A Honk and Seek Adventure]] if the player had damaged it at least once. Unlike the regular miniboss drops, it had no minimum damage requirement. Only one can be found per event.
## Usage
A Ragna Rock can be placed on the player's [[Private Island]] as decoration.
## Trivia
- The Ragna Rock is a reference to the [[Ragnarock]].
  - Additionally, it has a golden axe in its texture, further referencing the weapon, which was called Ragnarock Axe at the time.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Apr 1, 2025|Added Ragna Rock.|

# Reforge Anvil
Categories: Legacy items, Epic items, Items, Reforging
Legacy Item This item is no longer obtainable other than trading with players.
[Reforge Anvil]
Type: Block
Rarity: EPIC ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 2,000,000 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: REFORGE_ANVIL
Tooltip: Legacy Item; This anvil has had its functionality merged with Anvils. Apply Reforge Stones and other items with an Anvil!; Can be sold in Shops for 2,000,000; Coins!; EPIC
A Reforge Anvil was an [[Rarity|EPIC]] item. It could [[Reforging|reforge]] items on the [[Private Island]].
## Obtaining
### [[Shops|Merchant]]
A Reforge Anvil could be bought from the [[Smithmonger]] for 2,000,000 [[Coins]]. One could be purchased every [[Time Systems|SkyBlock Year]].
## Usage
The player could open the Reforge Anvil menu by interacting with it or its associated NPC. It allowed players to reforge items with [[Reforge Stones]] and access the [[Blacksmith]]'s interface.
### Trading
[[Reforge Anvil]] 1 Reforge Anvil can be sold in shops for 2,000,000 [[Coins]].
### NPC
The Reforge Anvil was accompanied by an NPC that could be interacted with to access the same menu. The NPC would have one of the following names: Show/Hide Names
- Alice
- Camille
- Chloé
- Iris
- Jeanne
- Lou
- Léa
- Manon
- Margaux
- Marie
- Mathilde
- Maxime
- Nina
- Océane
- Pauline
- Sarah
## Tips
- [[Malik]] in the [[Dungeon Hub]] and [[Catacombs Entrance]], the [[Blacksmith]] in the [[Hub Island|Hub]] or the advanced anvil near the [[Smithmonger]] used to do the same actions as the Reforge Anvil, making them an alternative.
- The NPC associated with a Reforge Anvil
- Reforge Anvil GUI
## Trivia
- Most of the names for the Reforge NPCs were French.
  - This may have been because Hypixel itself is from Québec, the only Canadian province where French is the sole official language.
- If the player had [[Seal of the Family]], buying a Reforge Anvil from the [[Smithmonger]] and selling it back used to result in a profit of 60,000 [[Coins]].
- Reforge Anvils were discontinued on March 31, 2026 in the 0.24.3 patch and their functionality was taken over by normal [[Anvil|Anvils]].
  - Although after discontinuation Reforge Anvils cannot be placed on [[Private Island]], blocks which were placed before the update may remain.
    - Their accompanying NPCs have disappeared with the update, though, and the blocks are no longer functional.
    - Clicking on a Reforge Anvil block sends the message Reforge Anvils have had their functionality replaced with Anvils. You may break this block and sell it to an NPC! in chat.
  - A pre-discontinuation Reforge Anvil in inventory:

|Mar 17, 2026|Removed crafting recipe.|
|Mar 17, 2026|Added a stock limit of 1 per SkyBlock Year.|
|Mar 17, 2026|Added an NPC sell price of 2,000,000 [[Coins]].|
|Mar 31, 2026|0.24.3|Reforge Anvil can no longer be purchased from the [[Smithmonger]].|
|Mar 31, 2026|0.24.3|Reforge Anvil has its functionality replaced by Anvils ([[Anvil]]).|
|Mar 31, 2026|0.24.3|Reforge Anvil can no longer be auctioned or traded|

A Rusty Spoon is a [[Rarity|SPECIAL]] [[Mementos|Memento]].
## Obtaining
A Rusty Spoon could be bought from the [[Corrupt Goon]] in the [[Hub Island|Hub]] for 10,000 [[Coins]] while [[Candidate Aura|Aura]] is elected [[Mayor Election|Mayor]].
## Usage
The [[Rusty Spoon]] Rusty Spoon allowed the player to assist in breaking the [[Prison of the Bereft]] added by [[Candidate Aura|Aura]].
The Rusty Spoon has two custom [[Stats]], +1% Tetanus Chance and +2 Determination. Tetanus Chance grants a chance for the player to lose 70 [[Health]] when hitting [[Prison of the Bereft]]. Additionally, losing health granted the following message in chat:
TETANUS! The metal exacts it's price. Rust now flows through your veins...

# Shiny Relic

Also known as: Pig Relic

[Item Properties]

Tradeable: ✔ Yes

Item ID: SHINY_RELIC
Tooltip: A perfect replica of the Shiny Relic deposited on the Blood God Shrine following the Dante fight.; Exceedingly rare item!; Found by: PLAYER Date: YYYY-MM-DD; SPECIAL
Shiny Relic is a [[Rarity|SPECIAL]] [[Mementos|Memento]], obtained from [[Shiny Orb|Shiny Orbs]]. When released, its drop chance was said to be about 1/20,000,000 (0.000005%). However, this was likely later changed to ensure all Shiny Relics were found within a reasonable amount of time.
## Usage
When 7 Shiny Relics are collected, they are used at the [[Blood God Shrine]] to summon the [[Ancient Pig]]. They currently have no known use beyond this purpose.
When a Shiny Relic is found, it is automatically submitted to the [[Blood God Shrine]]. The player who finds a Shiny Relic will maintain possession of a unique copy of the item.
## Relics Found
|Relic #|Found By|Date Found|
|-|-|-|
|1|carten7|April 17, 2021|
|2|niszczyciel149|April 18, 2021|
|3|The_Blood_Angel|April 21, 2021|
|4|JCRobertson|April 24, 2021|
|5|Jimmicim|April 29, 2021|
|6|lzyy_|April 30, 2021|
|7|MrLopaiz|May 7, 2021|
## Types
Each Shiny Relic is specific and has its own head. Note the amount of dots on the faces of the relic.
## Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|About 2 minutes after a Relic is found|WOAH! <username> found Shiny Relic #X, gg!! The relic has been deposited on the Blood God Shrine!; The shrine is empowered, but who knows what it means...|
|The seventh and final Relic is found|The 7th Shiny Relic has been found by [VIP] MrLopaiz! The Blood God Shrine is agitated and brewing with power!|
## Trivia
- Despite the relics being [[Drop Chance|11RNGesus Incarnate]] drops, the [[Blood God Shrine]] seems to suggest they're [[Drop Chance|9Pray RNGesus]] drops.
- The [[Ancient Pig]] that will be summoned from the [[Blood God Shrine]] was hinted at as one of [[Resistance Leader|Technoblade's]] perks when the [[Resistance Leader]] election winners showed up in the [[Dark Auction]].
- The Shiny Relics are most likely a reference to the Dragon Balls.

# Sloth Hat of Celebration
Aliases: Cheeky Sloth Hat of Celebration, Cool Sloth Hat of Celebration, Cute Sloth Hat of Celebration, Derp Sloth Hat of Celebration, Flushed Sloth Hat of Celebration, Grumpy Sloth Hat of Celebration, Happy Sloth Hat of Celebration, Regular Sloth Hat of Celebration, Shock Sloth Hat of Celebration, Sloth Hat, Sloth Hat Of Celebration, Tears Sloth Hat of Celebration
Categories: Legacy items, Special items, Accessories, Armor
Legacy Item This item is no longer obtainable other than trading with players.
[Sloth Hat of Celebration]
Type: Hatcessory ([[Hatcessories]])
Rarity: SPECIAL ([[Rarity]])
Source: [[Simon]]
[Item Properties]
Upgradeable: ✕ No
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✔ Yes
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: PARTY_HAT_SLOTH
Tooltip: Magic Find: +1; Ability: Party Time!; Gain +1&#xE003; Intelligence while on your head for each SkyBlock year you've been playing.; Obtained: 2023; SPECIAL HATCESSORY
The Sloth Hat of Celebration is a [[Rarity|SPECIAL]] [[Hatcessories|Hatcessory]] that was given out during the 4th [[SkyBlock Anniversary]].
## Obtaining
The Sloth Hat of Celebration was distributed by [[Simon]] during the 4th [[SkyBlock Anniversary]] in front of the [[Bazaar]].
### Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Collecting Sloth Hat|You claimed a [Expression] Sloth Hat of Celebration!|
|Another player collects Sloth Hat|Simon gave a [Expression] Sloth Hat of Celebration to <player>!|
|Attempting to claim another Sloth Hat|[NPC] Simon: I've already given you a Sloth Hat for this year! Happy Anniversary!|
## Usage
The Sloth Hat of Celebration grants +1 [[Magic Find]]. It additionally provides +1 [[Intelligence]] for each [[Time Systems|SkyBlock Year]] played when worn. The [[Magic Find]] bonus can only be obtained once from [[Hatcessories]].
When stored in the player's [[Accessory Bag]], the Sloth Hat of Celebration will count for [[Accessory Power]]. All [[Hatcessories]] count for the same [[Accessory Power]], meaning one of any kind will count for the same as all available ones combined.
## Trivia
- The expression of the hat was randomly selected when it was claimed, like the color of [[Century Cakes|Century Cake]] and [[Crab Hat of Celebration]].
- The Grumpy expression is a reference to Grumpy Cat, a popular meme from the 2010's.
- Players could only acquire a Sloth Hat on one [[Profile]].
- The Sloth Hats, [[Crab Hat of Celebration|Crab Hats]], and the [[Pulse Ring]] are the only [[Accessories]] that give [[Magic Find]] without [[Enrichments]].
- The amount of [[Intelligence]] gained depends only on the number of [[Time Systems|SkyBlock years]] that have passed since the player created their oldest profile, regardless of the player's activity during this time. The current maximum, which applies to players who joined during the first SkyBlock year, is 521? (Early Autumn 30th, 522, 10:51 AM | Click to Purge the Cache).
- Despite one of Hypixel's owners being associated with sloths, this is the first Sloth themed item in the game.
- [[Hatcessories|Hatccessories]] are inconsistent with their names. The 2022 Crab Hats are the only Hatcessories specifying their year of origin in their title. Crab Hats and Sloth Hats are said to be "of Celebration", while the Balloon Hats are "Anniversary".
- Sloth Hats come in 10 variants, while every other [[Hatcessories]] only has 9.
## Gallery

- Cool Sloth Hat of Celebration.png

- Tears Sloth Hat of Celebration.png

Item ID: SNOW_UNION
Tooltip: Place this minion and it will start generating and shoveling snow!; Requires an open area to place snow. Minions also work when you are offline!; However, it seems like this minion is on strike.; Time Between Actions: FOREVER!; Max Storage: 64; Resources Generated: 0; Obtained from the A Honk and Seek Adventure event during April Fools.; SPECIAL
The Snow Union I is a [[Rarity|SPECIAL]] Item which was obtained during [[A Honk and Seek Adventure]].
## Obtaining
The Snow Union I had a 1% chance to be found from [[Gifts]] during [[A Honk and Seek Adventure]]. Only one can be found per event.
## Usage
Trying to place the Snow Union I like a [[Minions|Minion]] on the [[Private Island]] will result in a random message in the player's chat. This can be done even if there are no minion slots available.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Random Dialogue|
|-|-|
|None|[MINION] Snow Minion I: We need two days off a week!|
|None|[MINION] Snow Minion I: I refuse to work in such conditions!|
|None|[MINION] Snow Minion I: If you can go offline, we want to do it too!|
|None|[MINION] Snow Minion I: Pay us in Coins first!|
## Trivia
- The Snow Union I is a reference to [[Snow Minion|Snow Minion I]].
  - Additionally, it also jokes about how [[Minions]] are forced to constantly work for players.
  - It could also refer to [[Free Will]], which grants minions the choice to either abandon their players or stay and work.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Apr 1, 2025|Added Snow Union I.|

# Staff of the Rising Sun
Aliases: Staff Of The Rising Sun, Staff of the rising sun
Categories: Legacy items, Epic items, Weapons, The Uprising, Wands
Legacy Item This item is no longer obtainable other than trading with players.
[Staff of the Rising Sun]
Type: Wand
Rarity: EPIC ([[Rarity]])
Source: Seraphine before the Dante fight
[Item Properties]
Enchantable: ✔ Yes
Reforgeable: ✕ No
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✔ Yes
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: HOPE_OF_THE_RESISTANCE; GENERALS_HOPE_OF_THE_RESISTANCE
Tooltip: Ability: Ray of Hope RIGHT CLICK Channels your energy towards the target location, exploding on impact for 750 damage.; Costs 10% of your total mana to use. This is the only item that can deal damage to Dante.; EPIC WAND
The Staff of the Rising Sun is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] Weapon which was obtained from the Resistance and was the only weapon that could deal damage to [[Dante]].
## Obtaining
Obtained from [[Clerk Seraphine|Seraphine]] (along with the [[Armor of the Rising Sun]]), after the [[Resistance Leader]] had been elected, and before [[Dante|The Uprising]] started. It was the only weapon that could deal damage to [[Dante]]. It is also one of the only weapons in the game that does not deal melee damage.
There was a player playtime requirement of 8 hours to collect this item.
## Usage
Wand:
Staff of the Rising Sun
[Effects]
[Ability: Ray of Hope; Channels your energy towards the target location, exploding on impact for 750 damage. Costs 10% of your total Mana to use. This is the only item that can deal damage to Dante.; Intelligence Scaling (Damage Calculation): 0.2]
## Bugs
- When used on [[Dirt]] or [[Grass]], it will leave the top part of the flower sitting on the dirt. Although this is only a visual bug, and the bugged flower will disappear when clicked.
## Trivia
- It has pull to aim, similar to the [[Spirit Sceptre]], which means that the ability will follow the player's crosshair.
- The weapon is classified as a wand, therefore it cannot be enchanted.
- Technoblade, the [[Resistance Leader]], owns a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] version of this wand, which only costs 2% of your total mana.
- Some players still have it as a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] rarity item, before it was changed to [[Rarity|EPIC]] to match the [[Armor of the Rising Sun|Armor of the Rising Sun's]] rarity.
- Players were able to obtain multiple sets of the Staff and [[Armor of the Rising Sun]] if they went back to Seraphine after the 0.11.117 minor update introducing the resistance_equipment content/internal advancement.
- The [[Resistance General|Resistance Generals]] were given a special [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] version of the item, with the item ID being GENERALS_HOPE_OF_THE_RESISTANCE instead of the usual HOPE_OF_THE_RESISTANCE.
- If the player closed the menu before claiming the staff, they could log out and log back in to be able to claim it again.
- The Staff of the Rising Sun cannot be reforged or enchanted (except for [[Ultimate Wise]] and [[Vicious]], however, [[Ultimate Wise]] does not function.)
## Gallery
- EPIC In-hand model. [[Rarity|EPIC]] In-hand model.
- LEGENDARY Item model. [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] Item model.
- LEGENDARY In-hand model. [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] In-hand model.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Jun 24, 2026|Added a custom texture for Staff of the Rising Sun as part of the SkyBlock Resource Pack.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Apr 12, 2021|Added Staff of the Rising Sun.|
|Apr 12, 2021|Some players can collect the staff twice.|
|Apr 14, 2021|Rarity decreased by 1 tier (from LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]]) to EPIC ([[Rarity]])).|
|Apr 14, 2021|Base ability damage nerfed from 2,000 to 750.|
|Jul 8, 2026|0.26|Added a custom texture for Staff of the Rising Sun as part of the SkyBlock Resource Pack.|

[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 186,624x (2,916 stacks (186,624 items total)) [[Wheat]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: TIGHTLY_TIED_HAY_BALE
Tooltip: Legacy Item; This item is no longer used throughout the game, and can be exchanged in your Legacy Trades menu, accessed through \ legacytrades.; * Co-op Soulbound * RARE
Tightly-Tied Hay Bale is a [[Rarity|RARE]] crafting material, that used to be unlocked at [[Wheat|Wheat XI (11)]].
## Obtaining
Tightly-Tied Hay Bale can no longer be obtained. Prior to 0.20.8, it was obtainable through following methods:
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Wheat XI (11) ([[Wheat]])|144x (2 stacks plus 16 (144 in total)) [[Enchanted Hay Bale (Old)]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Hay Bale]] x16 ×9 → Tightly-Tied Hay Bale|
### Pests
Tightly-Tied Hay Bale had a 1% chance to drop from killing a [[Fly]] on [[The Garden]].
## Usage
### Trading
[[Tightly-Tied Hay Bale]] 1 Tightly-Tied Hay Bale can be traded for 8 [[Enchanted Hay Bale]] in the /legacytrades menu.
### Removed Usage
#### Crafting
Tightly-Tied Hay Bale was previously used to craft [[Euclid's Wheat Sickle|Euclid's Wheat Hoe (Rare)]] and [[Cropie Armor]].
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|1x Melon Helmet ([[Tater Armor]]) [[Tightly-Tied Hay Bale]] 1x Tightly-Tied Hay Bale 6x [[Box of Seeds]] 20x [[Cropie]]|Recipe: [[Cropie]] x10 ×2 + Tightly-Tied Hay Bale + [[Box of Seeds]] x3 ×2 + [[Tater Armor]] → [[Cropie Armor]]|
|None|[[Tightly-Tied Hay Bale]] 1x Tightly-Tied Hay Bale 1x Melon Chestplate ([[Tater Armor]]) 6x [[Box of Seeds]] 20x [[Cropie]] 45x [[Enchanted Baked Potato]]|Recipe: [[Cropie]] x10 ×2 + [[Tater Armor]] + [[Box of Seeds]] x3 ×2 + Tightly-Tied Hay Bale + [[Enchanted Baked Potato]] x15 ×3 → [[Cropie Armor]]|
|None|1x Melon Leggings ([[Tater Armor]]) [[Tightly-Tied Hay Bale]] 1x Tightly-Tied Hay Bale 6x [[Box of Seeds]] 20x [[Cropie]] 30x [[Enchanted Golden Carrot]]|Recipe: [[Cropie]] x10 ×2 + Tightly-Tied Hay Bale + [[Box of Seeds]] x3 ×2 + [[Tater Armor]] + [[Enchanted Golden Carrot]] x15 ×2 → [[Cropie Armor]]|
|None|1x Melon Boots ([[Tater Armor]]) [[Tightly-Tied Hay Bale]] 2x Tightly-Tied Hay Bale 20x [[Cropie]]|Recipe: [[Cropie]] x10 ×2 + Tightly-Tied Hay Bale ×2 + [[Tater Armor]] → [[Cropie Armor]]|
|None|1x Euclid's Wheat Hoe (Uncommon) ([[Euclid's Wheat Sickle]]) 256x (4 stacks (256 items total)) [[Jacob's Ticket]] [[Tightly-Tied Hay Bale]] 256x Tightly-Tied Hay Bale|Recipe: [[Jacob's Ticket]] x64 ×4 + Tightly-Tied Hay Bale x64 ×4 + [[Euclid's Wheat Sickle]] → [[Euclid's Wheat Sickle]]|
## Tips
- Killing [[Fly]] [[Pests]] had a 1% chance to yield a Tightly-Tied Hay Bale, increased by [[Farming Fortune]] and [[Crop Fortune|Wheat Fortune]], which also granted collection, therefore killing the Pests during [[Jacob's Farming Contest]] could result in much easier DIAMOND medals.
## Trivia
- Obtaining a Tightly-Tied Hay Bale from killing a [[Fly]] Pest would immediately max out the player's Wheat collection ([[Wheat|Wheat XI (11)]]).

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: VITAMIN_LIFE
Tooltip: Power Stone; Combine 99,999,999x of this stone at the Maxwell to permanently unlock the Unhealthy power.; At 250 Magical Power:; -324 Health; Unique Power Bonus:; -200 Health; Requires Combat Skill Level MCCXII!; Obtained from the A Honk and Seek Adventure event during April Fools.; SPECIAL POWER STONE
Vitamin Life is a [[Rarity|SPECIAL]] Item that was obtainable during [[A Honk and Seek Adventure]].
## Obtaining
Vitamin Life had a 4% (1 in 25) chance to drop from killing Healthy [[Catacombs Mobs|Dungeon Mobs]].
## Usage
Vitamin Life has no use, despite being a [[Power Stones|Power Stone]]. It "requires" [[Combat|Combat MCCXII (1212)]] and 99,999,999x of itself to unlock the Unhealthy power, granting -324 [[Health]] at 250 Accessory Power and having a Unique Power Bonus of -200 [[Health]].
## Trivia
- Vitamin Life is a reference to [[Vitamin Death]].
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Apr 1, 2025|Added Vitamin Life.|

The Warden Art had a 2% (1 in 50) chance to drop from killing any tier of [[Revenant Horror]].

Looking at a Warden Art while consuming a [[Fudge Mint Core]] after using the [[Jerry Helmet]] until the following message appeared, gave the player [[An Extremely Difficult Item For You To Find]]:
THINK! Think Jerry, THINK!

Tooltip: Given to special members of the community who assisted in the creation of the Official Hypixel Wiki!; Awarded To: [player]; From: [player]; Date: [date]; Right-Click to view contributions!; * Co-op Soulbound * SPECIAL
The Wiki Journal is a [[Rarity|SPECIAL]] item given to contributors of the Official Hypixel Wiki. When used, it displays wiki information about the user it was given to.
## UI
Page 1 of 1
Awarded to: [Player's Name] Pages Modified: [Number] Contributions: [Number] Main Areas: -[Categories] wiki.hypixel.net
## Trivia
- Wiki Journals were originally going to have editions, but were quickly removed after feedback was provided.
- Wiki Journals were polled to potentially be tradeable, but this concluded with them being untradeable instead.
- The Wiki Journal was originally considered to be named the Editor's Journal.

The Yeti Rod had a 2% chance to be obtained from killing a [[Yeti]].

Sacks: Small Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]]); Medium Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]]); Large Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: ALL_IN_ALOE
Tooltip: This bold plant grows bigger the longer you wait, but wait too long and it's gone. Time your harvest right for big rewards.; Analyze this Mutation at the Crop Analyzer in Jake's Underground Lab.; LEGENDARY MUTATION
An All-in Aloe is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Mutations|Mutation]].
## Obtaining
### Greenhouse
An All-in Aloe can grow in the [[Greenhouse]] if there are 6 [[Magic Jellybean]] and 2 [[PlantBoy Advance]] within 1 block of [[Sand]].
While growing, an All-in Aloe does not need water and has 27 Growth Stages. When placed near another crop, it increases yield by 20%.
All-in Aloe can be harvested at any Growth Stage. Depending on the Growth Stage, it grants extra [[All-in Aloe Fragment|All-in Aloe Fragments]], but also increases the chance to reset to 1st Growth Stage upon reaching a new one.
### Crafting
All-in Aloe can also be crafted with [[All-in Aloe Fragment|All-in Aloe Fragments]].
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|9x [[All-in Aloe Fragment]]|Recipe: [[All-in Aloe Fragment]] ×9 → All-in Aloe|
### Fragment Yield

For every 9 [[All-in Aloe Fragment|All-in Aloe Fragments]], the mutation grants [[All-in Aloe]] 1 All-in Aloe instead.
Higher stages offer higher raw drops, but lower drops per unit of time due to cumulative reset risk and longer growth duration. On average, it is best to harvest at Growth Stage 14 to maximize drops. However, for completion, Growth Stage 11 is better as it will grant [[All-in Aloe]] 2 All-in Aloe, enough for analysis and purchasing [[Rose Dragon Pet|Rose Dragon Egg]] from [[Ludleth]]. Realistically, if the intent is purely growing as many aloes as possible to maximize profit then it is recommended to harvest at some point past Growth Stage 14 due to the possibility of missing cycles (via being asleep or busy), as well as reducing cost of replanting mutations used to grow the aloe.
## Crop Drops
When harvested, All-in Aloe also drops 140 (2 stacks plus 12) [[Moonflower]], 140 [[Sunflower]], 140 [[Wheat]], and +1,120 [[Farming|Farming XP]].
## Usage
### Crop Analyzer
An All-in Aloe can be analyzed in the [[Crop Analyzer]] for 4,600,000 [[Coins]]. When analyzed, it grants 2,300 [[Copper]]. The first time analyzing a All-in Aloe grants an additional 1,500 [[Copper]].
### NPC Trading
[[All-in Aloe]] 1 All-in Aloe is needed to purchase a [[Rose Dragon Pet|Rose Dragon Egg]] from [[Ludleth]].
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Sun's Grasp]]|None|1x [[Ray of Helios]] 3x [[Godseed]] [[All-in Aloe]] 3x All-in Aloe 128x (2 stacks (128 items total)) [[Compacted Sunflower]]|Recipe: [[Godseed]] ×3 + [[Compacted Sunflower]] x64 ×2 + [[Ray of Helios]] + All-in Aloe ×3 → [[Sun's Grasp]]|
## Trivia
- All-in Aloe does not decay while growing or after being placed by the player.
- If one were to grow a stage 27 All-in Aloe, it would result in a profit of .
  - With 3000 [[Farming Fortune]] and all Yield buff, it would also provide 3,112x (48 stacks plus 40 (3,112 in total)) [[Enchanted Hay Bale]], 3,112x [[Compacted Moonflower]], and 3,112x [[Compacted Sunflower]], for another .

- All-in Aloe in the Greenhouse All-in Aloe in the [[Greenhouse]]

An Ammonite Pet is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Fishing]] [[Pets|Pet]].
## Obtaining
An Ammonite Pet can be forged in [[The Forge]] with 1 [[Helix Fossil]] and 300,000 [[Coins]] for 3 days. It requires [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain IV (4)]] to forge.
### Forging Tree
- [[Ammonite Pet]] 1 Ammonite Pet
  - 1 [[Helix Fossil]]
  - 300,000 (4,687 stacks plus 32) [[Coins]]

## Trivia
- The Ammonite Pet is most likely inspired by the Pokémon Omanyte, a Pokémon that can be revived from a Helix Fossil, as the names are very similar in pronunciation as well as being very similar in design.
  - The Omanyte Pokémon is based on the "Ammonoidea" or "Ammonite", an extinct marine mollusk animal that existed during the Jurassic and Cretaceous Period.
- An Ammonite Pet can get up to 25% [[Sea Creature Chance]] by leveling to Level 100, holding a [[Hephaestus Souvenir]], and getting [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain X (10)]].
- Although its first perk's [[Sea Creature Chance]] is added to the pet as base stats, its third perk's stats are not.
- The Expert Cave Fisher perk used to nullify the vanilla mechanic of doubled fishing time when the bobber is not under direct sky access. This was changed as said vanilla mechanic was removed.
## History

|Sep 6, 2022|Moved Not a Snail's perks to Gift of the Ammonite.|
|Sep 6, 2022|Replaced Not a Snail with Expert Cave Fisher.|
|May 11, 2023|0.18.4|A fix that made Forge ([[The Forge]]) recipe text always show correctly.|
|Apr 9, 2024|0.20|Reduced the time to forge the Ammonite Pet from 12 days to 3.|

The Ananke Shard is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Wings of Destiny]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Kuudra
An Ananke Shard can be obtained from [[Kuudra|Kuudra Loot]].
### Fusion
An Ananke Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Special Fusion]]. For [[Attribute Fusion|Special Fusion]], the following input conditions must be met:
- First: [[Kraken Shard]]
- Second: Bird Family

## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Ananke Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Wings of Destiny]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Wings of Destiny ([[Attributes]]); Increases the odds of finding Ananke Feathers ([[Ananke Feather]]) by +2%–20%.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].

When viewed in the [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] or [[Bazaar]], the Ananke Shard has the following description:

An Ankylosaurus Pet can be forged in [[The Forge]] with 1 [[Gemstone|Flawless Opal Gemstone]] and 1 [[Clubbed Fossil]] for 7 days after donating a [[Clubbed Fossil]] to [[Dr. Stone]]. It requires [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain VII (7)]] to forge.

## Tips
- Neither Unyielding nor Clubbed Tail provide enough consistent value to be considered for this pet's viability, they should be treated as a bonus rather than something to rely on.
- This pet, ever since it was buffed, is one of the strongest mid-game combat pets outside of dungeons. The Armored Tank perk usually directly translates to +500 [[Strength]], which is a far more significant damage buff compared to what is provided by other pets at this point in progression. As the pet does not have a particularly high cost, most players who can reach 1000 [[Defense]] consistently should get this pet to improve their damage.
  - However, it does eventually get outclassed by the [[Ender Dragon Pet|Ender]] and [[Golden Dragon Pet]], both of which provide greater damage via additive damage multipliers or simply better stats.
- Putting [[Fuming Potato Book|Fuming Potato Books]] on armor makes hitting 1000 [[Defense]] easier.
- As this pet provides disproportionally high amounts of [[Strength]], using a Power Stone such as [[Luxurious Spool|Silky]] or [[Magma Urchin|Hurtful]] is suggested to balance [[Strength]] and [[Crit Damage]] easier, as well as using [[Dragon Claw|Fabled]] as the weapon reforge rather than [[Wither Blood|Withered]].
- Though it may seem appealing, using this pet with a defensive build is not very helpful as it won't fix the terrible offensive stats that comes from an armor with zero bonus damage. Instead, pairing this pet with a pre-existing damage build, such as 3/4 [[Crimson Armor]] and a [[Tarantula Armor|Tarantula Helmet]] (as the most budget example) would provide a far greater impact overall
  - If survivability is an issue, using the [[Reaper Mask]] is a viable option, as its main drawback of not boosting damage can be partially mitigated with this pet.
- As the cap to bonus [[Strength]] does not change in dungeons, it is not suggested to use this pet in dungeons as its other perks are not impactful enough to make it viable. Instead, a defensive pet such as [[Turtle Pet]] or a utility pet such as [[Black Cat Pet]] is generally better around mid-game.
  - A strictly damage bonus alternative in dungeons is the [[Skeleton Pet]], which can greatly improve the damage output of shortbows such as [[Juju Shortbow]] far more than this pet can.

- Second: Any Shard

### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Apex Dragon Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Veteran]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Veteran ([[Attributes]]); Grants +1–10 [[Combat Wisdom]].
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].

When viewed in the [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] or [[Bazaar]], the Apex Dragon Shard has the following description:

Aquamarine Dye is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Dyes|Dye]].
## Obtaining
Aquamarine Dye has a variable chance to drop when killing [[Aquatic]] [[Sea Creatures]]. It cannot drop when [[Loot Sharing]].
The drop chance of this [[Dyes|Dye]] may be temporarily doubled or tripled by [[Vincent]].

## Usage
Aquamarine Dye can be applied to any vanilla [[Armor]] piece by combining the two items in an [[Anvil]] to change the hex color of that piece to ⁠ #7FFFD4. It costs 100 [[Bits]] to combine the two items.
It can also be donated to [[Vincent]] to obtain a [[Bucket of Dye]] with a texture unique to its color.

# AquaMaster™ Hydromax

Item ID: AQUAMASTER_HYDROMAX
Tooltip: Use it on plants in the Greenhouse to keep them watered.; Ability: Hydrate HOLD RIGHT CLICK Aim and hold in the direction of a plant to water it. Dispenses 350; water per second.; Ability: Fill RIGHT CLICK When aiming at a water source, fills your can back up with water.; Ability: Aquaburst EX Spreads water in a larger area at once.; Water: 0\ 20k; LEGENDARY WATERING CAN
An AquaMaster™ Hydromax is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Watering Cans|Watering Can]].
## Obtaining
An AquaMaster™ Hydromax can be purchased from [[The Garden|SkyMart]] after reaching [[Crop Analyzer]] Milestone IV (20 [[Mutations|crops]] analyzed) requiring 2,500 [[Copper]], 1 [[AquaMaster™ X]], and 1 [[Iridium]].
## Usage
An AquaMaster™ Hydromax can be used to water crops in the [[Greenhouse]] in a 3x3 area. When used, it waters crops at a rate of 350 water per second. It has a water capacity of 20,000 and can be refilled by right-clicking [[Vanilla Objects/Blocks|Water]].

Tooltip: Decoration item; Obtained during the Carnival for placing within the Top 500 in Catch a Fish.; LEGENDARY
An Aquarium Diorama is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] item reward from the [[Carnival]]. The player is awarded a Aquarium Diorama after the event ends if the player ended in the Top 500 in the [[Carnival|Catch a Fish]] minigame.
## Obtaining
An Aquarium Diorama can be obtained after the [[Carnival]] disappears by placing within the Top 500 in [[Carnival|Catch a Fish]] minigame.
## Usage
An Aquarium Diorama can be placed on the player's [[Private Island]] as a decoration block.
## See Also

- [[Jar of Sand]]

An Archery Cube is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] item reward from the [[Carnival]]. The player is awarded an Archery Cube after the event ends if the player ended in the Top 500 in the [[Carnival|Zombie Shootout]] minigame.
## Obtaining
### Carnival
An Archery Cube can be obtained from the [[Carnival]] for placing within the Top 500 in the [[Carnival|Zombie Shootout]] minigame.
## Usage
An Archery Cube can be placed on the [[Private Island]].
## Trivia
- Despite the Archery Cube's name, Zombie Shootout is done with a [[Carnival Dart Tube]] rather than a [[Bows|Bow]].

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: DYE_ARCHFIEND
Tooltip: Combinable in Anvil; Changes the color of an armor piece to #B80036!; LEGENDARY DYE
Archfiend Dye is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Dyes|Dye]].
## Obtaining
An Archfiend Dye has a 0.015% (1 in 6,666) chance to be obtained by rolling an [[Archfiend Dice]] or a 0.15% (1 in 666) chance by rolling a [[High Class Archfiend Dice]]. The drop chance of this [[Dyes|Dye]] may be temporarily doubled or tripled by [[Vincent]].
## Usage
Archfiend Dye can be applied to any vanilla [[Armor]] piece by combining the two items in an [[Anvil]] to change the hex color of that piece to ⁠ #B80036. It costs 100 [[Bits]] to combine the items.
It can also be donated to [[Vincent]] to obtain a [[Bucket of Dye]] with a texture unique to its color.

Armor of Divan is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Mining]]-focused [[Armor|Armor Set]]. Up to 5 [[Gemstone Chamber]] can be applied to each piece to allow [[Gemstone|Gemstones]] to be applied.
## Obtaining
Armor of Divan can be forged in [[The Forge]] once the player has reached [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain VI (6)]]. Each piece takes 24 hours to forge.

## Upgrading
### Standard Modifiers
|Slot|Name|Description|
|-|-|-|
|[[Enchantment Table]]|[[Enchantments]]|[[Enchantments]] can be applied to Armor of Divan.|
|[[Anvil]]|Reforges ([[Reforging]])|Reforges ([[Reforging]]) can be applied to Armor of Divan through the [[Blacksmith]] or [[Reforge Stones]].|
|[[Recombobulator 3000]]|[[Recombobulator 3000]]|A [[Recombobulator 3000]] can be applied to Armor of Divan to increase its [[Rarity]] by +1. This increases the stats granted by the currently applied Reforge ([[Reforging]]) and Gemstones ([[Gemstone]]).|
|[[Gemstone]]|Gemstones ([[Gemstone]])|Gemstones ([[Gemstone]]) can be applied to Armor of Divan through the Gemstone Grinder ([[Geo]]) to grant stat buffs.|
### Unique Modifiers
A [[Gemstone Chamber]] is needed to unlock each [[Gemstone Slot]] on Armor of Divan. They are applied in the [[Geo|Gemstone Grinder]] at [[Geo]] instead of an [[Anvil]].
A [[Divan's Powder Coating]] can be applied to Armor of Divan, granting +10 [[Mining Fortune]]. This totals to +40 [[Mining Fortune]] when applied to all 4 pieces.

## Tips
- When using [[Gemstone Chamber|Gemstone Chambers]] to unlock Gemstone Slot, always unlock [[Gemstone|Topaz]] Slot, then [[Gemstone|Jade]] Slots and finally [[Gemstone|Amber]] Slots. However, if the armor is used exclusively for non-gemstones, prioritize [[Gemstone|Amber]] and [[Gemstone|Jade]] Slots over [[Gemstone|Topaz]] Slots.
## Trivia
- The Helmet of Divan appears to be a recolored version of the [[Warden Helmet]].
- Armor of Divan, [[Yog Armor]] and [[Sorrow Armor]] are the only armor sets that can hold [[Gemstone|Topaz]] Gemstones.
  - It is also the only armor with a Topaz slot specifically, as the other two sets allow Topaz application through the Universal [[Gemstone Slot]].
- Wearing a full set of Armor of Divan grants the player the [[Achievements|Memories of a Bal]] achievement.
- Other than the [[Shimmersparkle Chestplate]] and [[Talisman of Power]] and its upgrades, Armor of Divan pieces are tied for having the most gemstone slots with [[Mastiff Armor]], the [[Divan's Drill]] and the [[Gemstone Gauntlet]].

A maximum of 5 Gemstone Chambers can be applied to each set of Divan Armor, allowing 2 Jade ([[Gemstone]]), 2 Amber ([[Gemstone]]), and 1 Topaz ([[Gemstone]]) to be applied to each. The order of unlocking gemstone slots cannot be changed.|

# Artifact of Control
Aliases: Artifact Of Control
Categories: Legendary items, Accessories
[Artifact of Control]
Type: Accessory ([[Accessories]])
Rarity: LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: ARTIFACT_OF_CONTROL
Tooltip: Accessory Power: +16; Holding this artifact will grant you 2x voting power in Mayor Elections!; Works while in Accessory Bag!; LEGENDARY ACCESSORY
The Artifact of Control is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Accessories|Accessory]] that can be obtained from [[Shen's Auction]].
## Obtaining
### Shen's Auction
The Artifact of Control appears in [[Shen's Auction]], and can be won if the player is one of the top 20 bidders on the item when the auction ends. [[Ironman|♲ Ironman]] profiles have a separate auction, where top 10 bidders get the item. When [[Diaz|Mayor Diaz]] is elected with the Volume trading perk, the quantity is doubled to 40 top bids (20 for [[Ironman|♲ Ironman]])
### Raffle of the Century
An Artifact of Control can also be obtained as a MAJOR reward from [[Raffle of the Century]].
## Usage
Accessory:
Artifact of Control
[Effects]
[Holding this artifact will grant you 2x voting power in Mayor Elections (Mayor Election)!]
The Artifact of Control gives 2x the amount of votes when voting in a [[Mayor Election]]. This multiplier stacks with the player's [[Fame Ranks|Fame Rank]].

# Aspect of the Dragons

[Item Properties]

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: ASPECT_OF_THE_DRAGON
Tooltip: Damage: +225; Strength: +100; Gemstones: []; Ability: Dragon Rage RIGHT CLICK All Monsters in front of you take 12,000; damage. Hit monsters take large knockback.; Mana Cost: 100; This item can be reforged!; ❣ Requires Combat Skill 18.; LEGENDARY SWORD
The Aspect of the Dragons is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Swords|Sword]]. It is obtained as a drop from most [[Ender Dragon|Ender Dragons]].
## Obtaining
An Aspect of the Dragons has a chance to drop from all [[Ender Dragon|Ender Dragons]], excluding [[Ender Dragon|Superior Dragons]]. The following formula is used:
PlacedSummoningEyes*3%
## Usage
Sword:
Aspect of the Dragons [[Damage]]: +225 [[Strength]]: +100
[Effects]
[Ability: Dragon Rage RIGHT CLICK; All Monsters in front of you take 12,000 damage. Hit monsters take large knockback.; Mana Cost (Intelligence): 100; Intelligence Scaling (Damage Calculation): 0.1]
The Aspect of the Dragons' Dragon Rage ability summons a cone of fire particles 7.5 blocks long and 60° wide: all enemies within the cone take 12,000 damage and take large knockback. This ability costs 100 [[Mana]] and has no cooldown. [[Slayer]] bosses, [[Mythological]] mobs, [[Aquatic]] mobs, and [[Magmatic]] mobs are unaffected by the ability.
Multiple ways to increase the stats of the Aspect of the Dragons exist:
- The [[Dragonfuse Glove]] buffs the Aspect of the Dragons by +35 [[Damage]] and +50 [[Strength]], as well as reducing knockback dealt from Dragon Rage.
- The [[Ender Dragon Pet]] grants +50 [[Damage]] and +30 [[Strength]] to the Aspect of the Dragons.
## Essence Crafting
Essence Required; [[Dragon Essence]]:
|Action|Cost|Cumul. (Cumulative)|
|-|-|-|
|Conversion (The cost of the conversion to a dungeon item.)|150|150|
|✪|50|200|
|✪✪|100|300|
|✪✪✪|200|500|
|✪✪✪✪|300; 10,000 [[Coins]]|800; 10,000 [[Coins]]|
|✪✪✪✪✪|400; 25,000 [[Coins]]|1,200; 35,000 [[Coins]]|
## Trivia
- The Aspect of the Dragons references the Dragon Sword in UHC Champions, another Hypixel minigame: the Dragon Sword itself likely references the Aspect of the Dragon buff from League of Legends.
- The Dragon Rage ability used to work on [[Ender Dragon|Ender Dragons]], causing them to take extreme knockback and leave the [[Dragon's Nest]]. The boss bar would remain [[Summoning Eye|Summoning Eyes]] could not be placed, and [[Gregory]] would also remain. This was patched in a later update.
  - This ability also used to work on [[Sea Creatures]] before Update 0.9.10, allowing griefers to knock rare mobs, such as [[Great White Shark|Great White Sharks]] and [[Yeti|Yetis]] into their despawn range or the void.
- The [[Lost Adventurer]] mini-boss in [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]] attacks with an Aspect of the Dragons and occasionally uses the Dragon Rage ability: however, it is displayed as Dragon's Breath.

[Item Metadata]

Tooltip: Gear Score: 985 (1080); Defense: +250 (+275); True Defense: +20 (+20); Damage: +270 (+297); Strength: +150 (+165); Ferocity: +30 (+30); Intelligence: +50 (+55); Gemstones: [☤] [⚔]; Deals +50% damage to  Wither mobs.; Grants +1  Damage and +2  Defense per Catacombs level.; Right-click to use your class ability!; This item can be reforged!; ❣ Requires The Catacombs Floor VII Completion.; LEGENDARY DUNGEON SWORD
The Astraea is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Swords|Sword]]. It is unlocked upon completion of [[The Catacombs - Floor VII]].
The Astraea is one of the four [[Necron's Blade (Unrefined)|Necron's Blade]] variants, alongside the [[Hyperion]], [[Valkyrie]], and [[Scylla]], focusing on [[Defense]], [[True Defense]] and [[Tank]] builds.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|8 [[Jolly Pink Rock]] One of the following: [[Necron's Blade (Unrefined)]] [[Scylla]] [[Hyperion]] [[Valkyrie]]|Recipe: [[Jolly Pink Rock]] ×8 + [[Necron's Blade (Unrefined)]]/[[Scylla]]/[[Hyperion]]/[[Valkyrie]] → Astraea|
## Usage
Sword:
Astraea [[Defense]]: +250 [[True Defense]]: +20 [[Damage]]: +270 [[Strength]]: +150 [[Ferocity]]: +30 [[Intelligence]]: +50
[Effects]
[Deals +50% damage to Wither mobs.; Grants +1 Damage and +2 Defense per Catacombs level.; Right-click to use your class ability!]
### Crafting
The Astraea can be crafted into another [[Necron's Blade (Unrefined)|Necron's Blade]] variant using the corresponding recipe, keeping all enchantments and upgrades. Items used in the crafting recipe are consumed.
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Valkyrie]]|None|8 [[Diamante's Handle]] One of the following: [[Necron's Blade (Unrefined)]] [[Scylla]] [[Hyperion]] [[Astraea]] Astraea|Recipe: [[Diamante's Handle]] ×8 + [[Necron's Blade (Unrefined)]]/[[Scylla]]/[[Hyperion]]/Astraea → [[Valkyrie]]|
|[[Hyperion]]|None|8x [[L.A.S.R.'s Eye]] One of the following: [[Necron's Blade (Unrefined)]] [[Scylla]] [[Astraea]] Astraea [[Valkyrie]]|Recipe: [[L.A.S.R.'s Eye]] ×8 + [[Necron's Blade (Unrefined)]]/[[Scylla]]/Astraea/[[Valkyrie]] → [[Hyperion]]|
|[[Scylla]]|None|8 [[Bigfoot's Bola]] One of the following: [[Necron's Blade (Unrefined)]] [[Hyperion]] [[Astraea]] Astraea [[Valkyrie]]|Recipe: [[Bigfoot's Bola]] ×8 + [[Necron's Blade (Unrefined)]]/[[Hyperion]]/Astraea/[[Valkyrie]] → [[Scylla]]|
It can have [[Necron's Blade Scrolls]] applied to it, granting their effects on right click.
## Trivia
- The Astraea references the Greek goddess of innocence and justice.
- The Astraea is the only weapon that grants [[True Defense]].
- The Astraea and [[Scylla]] are tied for granting the most unique stats, at six.
- Donating an Astraea to the Museum gives credit for [[Necron's Blade (Unrefined)|Necron's Blade]].

Item ID: RETIA_SUPREMA
Tooltip: Hunting Tool; Ability: Retentio RIGHT CLICK Traps can be placed near the spawn location of a Shard creature, with time it will eventually catch it.; Ability: Suprema Reduces the time to capture by 50%.; Suprema inter retia, nulla praeda elabitur.; ❣ Requires Hunting Skill 20.; LEGENDARY TRAP
The Astral Huntrap is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Huntraps|Huntrap]], whose recipe is unlocked at [[Tender Wood|Tender Wood IX (9)]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Tender Wood IX (9) ([[Tender Wood]])|1x [[Greater Huntrap]] 4x [[Enchanted Tender Wood]] 128x [[Enchanted Fig Log]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Tender Wood]] ×4 + [[Enchanted Fig Log]] x32 ×4 + [[Greater Huntrap]] → Astral Huntrap|
## Usage
An Astral Huntrap can be placed near the spawn location of any creature that can drop a [[Attributes|Shard]] to collect one. The time to catch a shard is random and based on the rarity of the shard caught (see [[Huntraps|Huntraps#Time]]).
## Trivia
- The text in the Huntrap's description, "Suprema inter retia, nulla praeda elabitur." is Latin, and means "Supreme among the traps, no prey shall escape."

- A placed Huntrap

The Atomsplit Katana is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Swords|Sword]]. It is unlocked at [[Enderman Slayer|Enderman Slayer VI (6)]]. It is the upgraded form of the [[Vorpal Katana]] and the final form of the [[Voidwalker Katana]].

Tooltip: Health: +160; Defense: +50; Intelligence: +275; Ability Damage: +16%; Gemstones: [⚔] [⚔]; Tiered Bonus: Arcane Energy (0\ 4); Every 1s, dealing Magic Damage from an ability grants 1 stack of Ѫ Arcane Energy.; Lose 1 stack after 4s of not gaining a stack.; Ability: Homing Missiles SNEAK At 10 stacks, sneak to reset your stacks and shoot 3 homing missiles dealing 5,000 Damage each.; This item can be reforged!; ❣ Requires Combat Skill 22.; ❣ Requires Kuudra Basic Tier Completion.; LEGENDARY HELMET
Aurora Armor is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Armor]] set. There are 4 upgraded tiers of Aurora Armor: Hot, Burning, Fiery, and Infernal, which can all be obtained by prestiging the previous tier.
## Obtaining
Basic Tier Aurora Armor Pieces can be obtained from [[Kuudra|Paid Reward Chests]] at the end of a [[Kuudra]] fight.
Piece Drop Chances:
- Basic Tier: 4.31%
- Hot Tier: 4.2%
- Burning Tier: 4.05%
- Fiery Tier: 3.91%
- Infernal Tier: 4.08%
Higher tiers of Aurora Armor can be obtained by prestiging the previous tier at a blacksmith ( [[Malik]], [[Igor]], [[Alwin]], or [[The Hex]]), which can be done if it has 10 [[Star Upgrades|Stars]] applied to it. After being prestiged, Armor pieces lose their Stars.

[Effects]
[Tiered Bonus: Arcane Energy; Every 1s➜0.7➜0.5s , dealing Magic Damage from an ability grants 1 stack of Arcane Energy Ѫ.; At 10 stacks, sneak to reset your stacks and shoot 5 homing missiles dealing 5,000 Damage each.; Lose 1 stack after 4s of not gaining a stack.; Pieces Required: 2+]
Upon having at least 2 pieces equipped, the Tiered Set Bonus: Arcane Energy is unlocked. When the player deals [[Damage Calculation|Magic Damage]], they gain 1 stack, with a cooldown dependent on how many pieces of Aurora Armor are worn - 1s for wearing two pieces, 0.7s for wearing three, and 0.5s when wearing all four pieces. One stack is lost after 4s of not gaining a stack.
Upon reaching 10 stacks of Arcane Energy, the player is intended to be able to sneak to activate the Homing Missiles ability, firing missiles that home in to targets near them, similar to the [[Flower of Truth]]'s projectiles. They have a base of 5000 [[Damage Calculation|Magic Damage]] and 0.3x ability scaling. These missiles only hit one target each; they do not deal splash damage. 3 missiles are fired if wearing Basic tier Aurora Armor; 4 if using Hot or Burning tier; and 5 if using Fiery or Infernal tier.
Dealing [[Damage Calculation|Magic Damage]] grants Arcane Energy stacks as intended, but sneaking at 10 stacks currently does not activate Homing Missiles.

# Aurora Staff
Categories: Legendary items, Gemstone upgradeable items, Weapons, Swords, Kuudra
[Aurora Staff]
Type: Weapon ([[Weapons]])
Rarity: LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])
Combat Level ([[Combat]]) Requirement: Combat XXII (22) ([[Combat]]); [[Kuudra]] Basic Tier Completion

Gemstones: Sapphire Gemstone Slot (Cost to unlock 250,000 coins) Sapphire Gemstone Slot (Cost to unlock 250,000 coins)
[Item Properties]

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: RUNIC_STAFF
Tooltip: Damage: +20; Intelligence: +250; Gemstones: [] []; Runic Item Use with Aurora Armor! Cast Runic Zap while matching the rune of your targets.; Left-Click To Swap Rune Ability: Runic Zap RIGHT CLICK Fires a beam of energy, hitting the first enemy on its path for 10,000.; Deals reduced damage past 10; blocks.; Mana Cost: 10 Cooldown: 1s; This item can be reforged!; ❣ Requires Combat Skill 22.; ❣ Requires Kuudra Basic Tier Completion.; LEGENDARY SWORD
The Aurora Staff is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Swords|Sword]] that can be obtained from defeating Kuudra. Its ability, Runic Zap, fires a beam, similarly to the Mage's class passive Mage Staff in Dungeons.
## Obtaining
The Aurora Staff can be obtained from the paid [[Kuudra]] reward chest.
Drop Chances:
- Basic Tier: 3.45%
- Hot Tier: 3.36%
- Burning Tier: 3.24%
- Fiery Tier: 3.12%
- Infernal Tier: 3.27%
## Usage
Sword:
Aurora Staff [[Damage]]: +20 [[Intelligence]]: +250
[Effects]
[Runic Item; Use with Aurora Armor Aurora Armor! Cast Runic Zap while matching the rune of your targets.; Left-Click To Swap Rune Ability: Runic Zap RIGHT CLICK; Fires a beam of energy, hitting the first enemy on its path for 10,000. Deals reduced damage past 10 blocks.; Mana Cost (Intelligence): 10; Cooldown: 1s]
The Aurora Staff is a Mage-based weapon. It requires [[Combat|Combat XXII (22)]] and [[Kuudra]] Basic Tier Completion to use. It has different "modes", each representing a different rune. Because of this, it is meant to be used with [[Aurora Armor]], as it allows you to see the runic affinity of mobs. The beam it fires deals more damage if the runic affinity of a mob is the same as the rune on the staff. The three "modes" are:
- Virtuoso
- Mediator
- Defender
With the [[Aurora Armor]], different colored particles will appear, coming from the center of mobs. They will either be a flame particle (Mediator), an end particle (Virtuoso) or a cyan-colored particle (Defender). Using the correct "mode" will deal more [[Damage]]. If wearing the [[Aurora Armor]], after using the correct rune 10 times, each attack will also explode on impact.
## Trivia
- The Aurora Staff has 10,000 [[Ability Damage|Base Ability Damage]] and an [[Ability Damage|Ability Scaling]] of 0.20.
- The Aurora Staff's Damage decays at a rate of e^(-0.0225*Distance) per block.

|Floor VII ([[The Catacombs - Floor VII]]) (Master Mode)|Bedrock|10,000,000 [[Coins]]|0.6352% (1 in 157.4 runs)|1.116% (1 in 89.6 runs)|330|80|
## Usage
When the Auto Recombobulator is in a player's inventory or [[Accessory Bag]], its ability grants a 1% chance to automatically [[Recombobulator 3000|recombobulate]] [[Weapons]], [[Armor]], [[Tools]] and [[Accessories]] from mob drops. It has unlimited uses.
## Bugs
- An Auto Recombobulator can recombobulate a drop another player loot shared that the player with the Auto Recombobulator did not obtain themselves.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Nov 12, 2020|0.9.12|Added Auto Recombobulator.|
|Oct 20, 2021|Auto Recombobulator is now limited to armor, tools, weapons, and accessories instead of all items dropped from mobs.|

## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|1x [[Old Leather Boot]] 1x [[Condensed Lily Pad]] 1x [[Poison Sample]] 1x [[Water Orb]] 8x [[Alligator Skin]] 8x [[Busted Belt Buckle]] 16x [[Torn Cloth]] 16x [[Bronze Bowl]] 64x [[Rusty Coin]]|Recipe: [[Busted Belt Buckle]] x8 + [[Old Leather Boot]] + [[Alligator Skin]] x8 + [[Bronze Bowl]] x16 + [[Water Orb]] + [[Torn Cloth]] x16 + [[Condensed Lily Pad]] + [[Rusty Coin]] x64 + [[Poison Sample]] → Bayou Water Orb|
### Crafting Tree
- [[Bayou Water Orb]] 1 Bayou Water Orb 8 [[Busted Belt Buckle]] 1 [[Old Leather Boot]] 8 [[Alligator Skin]] 16 [[Bronze Bowl]] 1 [[Water Orb]] 16 [[Enchanted Raw Cod]] 2,560 (40 stacks) [[Raw Cod]] 8 [[Enchanted Sponge]] 1,280 (20 stacks) [[Sponge]] 16 [[Enchanted Raw Salmon]] 2,560 [[Raw Salmon]] 16 [[Enchanted Prismarine Shard]] 2,560 [[Prismarine Shard]] 16 [[Enchanted Lily Pad]] 2,560 [[Lily Pad]] 16 [[Enchanted Prismarine Crystals]] 2,560 [[Prismarine Crystals]] 16 [[Enchanted Clay Ball]] 2,560 [[Clay Ball]] 16 [[Enchanted Pufferfish]] 2,560 [[Pufferfish]] 16 [[Enchanted Tropical Fish]] 2,560 [[Tropical Fish]] 16 [[Torn Cloth]] 1 [[Condensed Lily Pad]] 160 (2 stacks plus 32) [[Enchanted Lily Pad]] 25,600 (400 stacks) [[Lily Pad]] 64 (1 stack) [[Rusty Coin]] 1 [[Poison Sample]]

|-|-|-|-|
|[[Enchanted Book]] (Bobbin' Time III ([[Bobbin' Time]]))|None|[[Bayou Water Orb]] 1x Bayou Water Orb 1x [[Winter Water Orb]] 1x [[Shark Water Orb]] 1x [[Hotspot Water Orb]] 1x [[Spooky Water Orb]] 1x [[Bobbin' Scriptures]] 1x [[Lava Water Orb]]|Recipe: Bayou Water Orb + [[Winter Water Orb]] + [[Spooky Water Orb]] + [[Bobbin' Scriptures]] + [[Shark Water Orb]] + [[Hotspot Water Orb]] + [[Lava Water Orb]] → [[Bobbin' Time]]|

A Bayou Water Orb can be given to [[Junker Joel]] in exchange for his [[Abiphones|Abiphone]] contact.

The Beach Ball Barn Skin is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Barn Skins|Barn Skin]] that could be obtained from a past [[Taylor|Seasonal Bundle]] from [[Taylor]].
## Obtaining
The Beach Ball Barn Skin could be purchased from the Summer 2024 [[Taylor|Seasonal Bundle]] from [[Taylor]]. Being [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]], it cost 650 [[SkyBlock Gems]]. However, due to being before the Winter 2024 Bundle, it cannot appear in future bundles, making it now unobtainable.
## Usage
The Beach Ball Barn Skin can be consumed on the [[The Garden|Garden]] and then used at [[The Garden|The Desk]] to change the look of the center plot.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jun 27, 2024|Added Beach Ball Barn Skin.|

Tooltip: Combinable in Anvil; Changes the color of an armor piece to #002FA7!; LEGENDARY DYE
Bingo Blue Dye is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Dyes|Dye]].
## Obtaining
The player can purchase a Bingo Blue Dye from the [[Bingo (NPC)|Bingo Shop]] at [[Bingo (NPC)|Bingo]] for 500 [[Bingo|Bingo Points]] once the player has reached Ⓑ Bingo Rank III (3).
## Usage
Bingo Blue Dye can be applied to any vanilla [[Armor]] piece by combining the two items in an [[Anvil]] to change the hex color of that piece to ⁠ #002FA7. It costs 100 [[Bits]] to combine the two items.
It can also be donated to [[Vincent]] to obtain a [[Bucket of Dye]] with a texture unique to its color.

The Black Widow Skin is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Helmet Skins|Helmet Skin]] that could be purchased from a [[Fire Sale]].
## Obtaining
The Black Widow Skin could be purchased from the [[Fire Sale]] hosted on October 23, 2020. Being [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]], it cost 650 [[SkyBlock Gems]] and had a limited stock of 1,500 purchases.
## Usage
The Black Widow Skin can be applied to a [[Tarantula Armor|Tarantula Helmet]] by combining it in an [[Anvil]] to change its appearance.
