[Item Metadata]
Item ID: BLAZETEKK_HAM_RADIO
Tooltip: Ability: Telecommunications If exactly two players within 110 blocks are on the same channel, both gain:; +60 Defense +40 True Defense +5☯ Combat Wisdom +10 Vitality Earn half stats while alone!; Ability: Tuning 4 Dummies RIGHT CLICK Switch to the next radio channel.; Your channel: 103.5 FM Nearby: 0 enjoyers; ☠ Requires Blaze Slayer 6.; LEGENDARY
The Blazetekk™ Ham Radio is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] item. It is unlocked at [[Blaze Slayer|Blaze Slayer LVL VI (6)]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Blaze Slayer VI (6) ([[Blaze Slayer]])|24x [[Inferno Vertex]] 64x [[Enchanted Redstone Lamp]] 96x (1 stack plus 32 (96 in total)) [[Molten Powder]]|Recipe: [[Molten Powder]] x16 ×6 + [[Inferno Vertex]] x12 ×2 + [[Enchanted Redstone Lamp]] x64 → Blazetekk™ Ham Radio|
## Usage
Item:
Blazetekk™ Ham Radio
[Effects]
[Ability: Telecommunications; If exactly two players within 110 blocks are on the same channel, both gain:; +60 Defense; +40 True Defense; +5 Combat Wisdom; +10 Vitality; Earn half stats while alone! Ability: Tuning 4 Dummies RIGHT CLICK; Switches to the next radio channel.]
The Blazetekk™ Ham Radio's Telecommunications ability grants the following buffs if exactly 2 players within a radius of 110 blocks are on the same channel:
- +60 [[Defense]]
- +40 [[True Defense]]
- +5 [[Combat Wisdom]]
- +10 [[Vitality]]
Both players gain this bonus when all conditions are met, and only half stats are gained when conditions are not met.
The Tuning 4 Dummies ability allows the Blazetekk™ Ham Radio to its radio channel. The channels available are:
|Channel|Description|
|-|-|
|... brz ...|610 AM|
|.. grz ... rrzz ..|S&R 40.5 FM|
|.. gblgb .. bz ..|SETI.930Mhz|
|... brz .. brz ....|1512 AM|
|- zoom -|Bluetooth 2.48 GHz|
|.. grchz ..|2325 XM|
|zzz|Infrared|
|... googl ...|GPS.1227Mhz|
|... ?|103.5 FM|
If the Blazetekk™ Ham Radio cannot find another player on the same channel, the following dialogue displays:
Your radio is weak. Find another enjoyer to boost it.
Finding another player on the same channel displays:
Your radio signal is strong!
If 3 or more players are on the same channel, following dialogue displays:
Your radio lost signal. There's too many enjoyers on this channel.
Additionally, the [[Bluetooth Ring]] and [[Bluertooth Ring]] grant an additional +3 and +4 [[Damage]] bonus respectively. The two bonuses do not stack with each other and are unaffected by signal strength.
## Trivia
- The Blazetekk™ Ham Radio's perks are similar to the [[Armor of the Pack]].
- If held in the hotbar, stepping on a pressure plate will rapidly cycle through the radio channels.

|Jun 21, 2023|Can now be donated to the [[Museum]].|
|Dec 3, 2024|0.20.9|Players now receive half of the buffs if they are the only one on that channel.|
|Dec 3, 2024|0.20.9|Doubled the Telecommunications range from 55 to 110|
|Dec 3, 2024|0.20.9|Quintupled some of the stats, it now gives +5 [[Combat Wisdom]] and +10 [[Vitality]]|
|Dec 3, 2024|0.20.9|Now warns the player if more than two players are in the same radio channel.|

Bone Dye can be applied to any vanilla [[Armor]] piece by combining the two items in an [[Anvil]] to change the hex color of that piece to ⁠ #E3DAC9. It costs 100 [[Bits]] to combine the two items.

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: BONE_BOOMERANG
Tooltip: Gear Score: 440 (480); Damage: +270 (+297); Strength: +130 (+143); Ability: Swing RIGHT CLICK Throw the bone a short distance, dealing the damage an arrow would.; Deals double damage when coming back. Pierces up to 10 foes.; This item can be reforged!; ❣ Requires The Catacombs Floor IV Completion.; LEGENDARY DUNGEON BOW
The Bonemerang is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Dungeons|Dungeon]] [[Bows|Bow]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|5x [[Spirit Bone]] 10x [[Enchanted Bone Block]] 15x [[Enchanted String]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted String]] x5 ×3 + [[Enchanted Bone Block]] x5 ×2 + [[Spirit Bone]] x5 → Bonemerang|
## Usage
Bow:
Bonemerang [[Damage]]: +270 [[Strength]]: +130
[Effects]
[Ability: Swing RIGHT CLICK; Throw bone a short distance, dealing the damage an arrow would.; Deals double damage when coming back. Pierces up to 10 foes.]
Bow:
[[Bonemerang]] [[Damage]]: +320 [[Strength]]: +150
[Effects]
[Ability: Swing RIGHT CLICK; Throw bone a short distance, dealing the damage an arrow would.; Deals double damage when coming back. Pierces up to 10 foes.]
The Bonemerang, unlike other bows, acts like a boomerang as its name suggests. Upon using the Bonemerang, it travels forward up to 13.5 blocks before reaching the apex of its journey and returning: it deals the wielder's ranged damage to mobs in its path, and deals double damage when returning.
If the Bonemerang comes into contact with a block or strikes more than 10 enemies while traveling, it will shatter and become unusable for 3 seconds: a Ghast Tear takes its place in the inventory. Other Bonemerangs can still be used when one is on cooldown.
## Upgrading
A Bonemerang can be upgraded into Bonemerang using 8 [[Thorn Fragment|Thorn Fragments]] to buff its stats, granting:
- +50 [[Damage]]
- +20 [[Strength]]
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|[[Bonemerang]] 1x Bonemerang 8x [[Thorn Fragment]]|Recipe: [[Thorn Fragment]] ×8 + Bonemerang → [[Bonemerang]]|
## Essence Crafting
Essence Required; [[Wither Essence]]:
|Action|Cost|Cumul. (Cumulative)|
|-|-|-|
|✪|20|20|
|✪✪|50|70|
|✪✪✪|100|170|
|✪✪✪✪|200; 10,000 [[Coins]]|370; 10,000 [[Coins]]|
|✪✪✪✪✪|300; 25,000 [[Coins]]|670; 35,000 [[Coins]]|
## Tips
- Throwing a Bonemerang at [[Thorn]] stuns him for 3-5 seconds, making it easier to shoot him with the [[Spirit Bow]].
  - If Thorn is stunned, he will increase in speed after the stun period, making it very hard to shoot them. This is likely because Thorn is designed in his AI to be faster every time damaged to dodge players' shots.
## Trivia
- The Bonemerang could be reference to Marowak's signature move in Pokémon.
- Players could swap the Bonemerang with a [[Death Bow]] and [[Edible Mace]] to deal millions of damage prior to the update that fixed sword-swapping.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jul 30, 2020|Added Bonemerang.|
|Mar 30, 2021|0.11.3|-50 [[Damage]] (from 320 to 270).|
|Apr 30, 2021|Bonemerang projectiles no longer inherit the properties of the held weapon, fixing [[Death Bow]] swapping.|
|May 7, 2021|Fixed Bonemerang damage doubling on each hit.|
|May 31, 2021|0.11.5|Fixed [[Soul Eater]] not working properly on Bonemerangs.|
|May 4, 2022|Fixed Bonemerangs being able to instantly kill Dropships in the [[Kuudra]] battle.|
|Sep 12, 2023|0.19.4|Added the upgraded Bonemerang.|

# Booster Cookie
Aliases: Booster Cookie (Untradable), Booster Cookies, Cookie Buff
Categories: Legendary items, Items, Pages with UIs, Bits Shop
[Booster Cookie]
Type: Consumable
Rarity: LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✕ No
Auctionable: ✕ No

[Shop]
Merchants: [[Elizabeth]]
Buy: 325 [[SkyBlock Gems]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: BOOSTER_COOKIE; FREE_COOKIE; REFUND_COOKIE; QUEST_COOKIE
Tooltip: Consume to gain the Cookie Buff for 4 days:; ‣ Ability to gain Bits!; ‣ +25☯ on all Wisdom stats ‣ +15 Magic Find ‣ Keep coins on death ‣ Permafly on private islands and gardens ‣ Quick access to some menus using their respective commands:; \ bazaar, \ bank, \ accessorybag, \ fishingbag, \ timepocket, \ anvil, \ hex, \ etable, \ potionbag, and \ quiver ‣ Sell items directly to the trades and cookie menu ‣ AFK immunity on your island and garden ‣ Toggle specific potion effects ‣ Link your items in chat using \ show ‣ Increases Chocolate Factory production by +0.25x ‣ Allows consuming Mixins directly from your inventory ‣ Call Abiphone Contacts with \ call; LEGENDARY
The Booster Cookie is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] consumable that grants multiple benefits that last for 4 days. Multiple cookies can be consumed at once to increase the duration.
## Obtaining
A Booster Cookie can be purchased from the [[Elizabeth|Community Shop]] for 325 [[SkyBlock Gems]].
[[Elizabeth]] will give every player one untradable Booster Cookie for free. This free booster cookie is available only once per account, not once per profile.
An additional Booster Cookie can also be obtained from her after completing the security quest in the [[Security Hall]], once per profile. These cookies cannot be sold on the [[Bazaar]], [[Auction House]] or to [[NPC|Merchants]]. They also cannot be stored in the player's [[Ender Chest]] or dropped on the ground, and can only be eaten by the player who obtained them.
Booster Cookie is the only item that can be bought from the [[Bazaar]] in all [[Profile|Profiles]].
## Effects
The Booster Cookie grants the player the following bonuses for 4 days:
- Ability to gain [[Bits]]! (See §Bits Available for more details)
- +25 on all [[Wisdom]] stats
- +15 [[Magic Find]]
- Keep [[Coins]] on death
- Permafly on private islands and gardens
- Quick access to some menus using their respective commands: /ah, /bazaar, /bank, /accessorybag, /timepocket, /anvil, /hex, /etable, /potionbag, and /quiver
- Sell items directly to the trades and cookie menu
- AFK immunity on your island and garden
- Toggle specific [[Potions|potion effects]]
- Link your items in chat using /show
- Insta-sell your Material stash to the [[Bazaar]]
- Increases [[Chocolate Factory]] production by +0.25x
- Allows consuming Mixins directly from your inventory
- Call Abiphone Contacts with /call
The Booster Cookie's duration counts down even when the player is offline.

## Bits Available
Booster Cookies allow players to generate [[Bits]], each Cookie has a limit to how many [[Bits]] can be generated, which changes with [[Fame Ranks]] and [[Museum/Milestones|Museum Milestones]]. Bits are generated every half an hour by 5.20833% (precisely 1/19.2) of what one Cookie can provide, which can be increased by [[Bits Talisman]]. Additionally, [[Bits]] are awarded from defeating Bosses, finishing dungeons or participating in Events across Skyblock. (See [[Bits|Earning Bits]] for more details)

## Trivia
- There is a purchase limit of 192 Booster Cookies per day.
- Disabling a [[Potions|Potion]] effect does not pause it; instead, it simply stops the effect from working on the player. Any duration timers will still tick down as normal, even when disabled.
- As part of compensation for the Hypixel Maintenance that occurred from 17 June 2021 to 22 June 2021, players who had a Cookie Buff active when the maintenance took place were compensated with an untradeable Booster Cookie via [[Jerry]].
  - These cookies, like the free cookie given to a player by [[Elizabeth]], cannot be sold to the [[Bazaar]], [[Auction House]] or to [[NPC|NPCs]] and cannot be dropped.
- Attempting to drop a non-normal cookie on the ground will tell the player You may not drop this Booster Cookie!.
- If the player was in a [[Co-op]] with player(s) who have not claimed the free cookie, they were able to get two or more free cookies by placing the free cookie(s) in a chest on their [[Private Island]].
  - This doesn't work anymore. The free Cookies are now tied to specific players. When another tries to consume it, the game sends this message: This cookie was given to another player! You may not consume it!
- A player can still obtain a Booster Cookie on [[Bingo]] through the Security Quest. However, because the player cannot spend Bits at [[Elizabeth]] on this profile, the only way they can be spent is through tuning templates with [[Maxwell]], or refreshing [[Experiments]].
  - This allows for the usual bonuses of a Booster Cookie, such as Wisdom, Magic Find, and flight.

Tooltip: Gear Score: 560 (616); Damage: +220 (+242); Strength: +300 (+330); Crit Damage: +50% (+55%); Gemstones: []; Ability: Petal Barrage RIGHT CLICK Fire a barrage of 3 roses that ricochets between enemies, hitting up to 5 foes. For every 10% of your health missing, your roses deal 2% more damage.; Health Cost: 250; Cooldown: 1s; This item can be reforged!; ❣ Requires The Catacombs Floor VI Completion.; LEGENDARY DUNGEON SWORD
The Bouquet of Lies is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] Dungeon [[Swords|Sword]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|1x [[Flower of Truth]] 12x [[Fel Rose]]|Recipe: [[Fel Rose]] x3 ×4 + [[Flower of Truth]] → Bouquet of Lies|
## Usage
Sword:
Bouquet of Lies [[Damage]]: +220 [[Strength]]: +300 [[Crit Damage]]: +50
[Effects]
[Ability: Petal Barrage RIGHT CLICK; Fires a Barrage of 3 roses that ricochets between enemies, hitting up to 5 foes. For every 10% of your health missing, your roses deal 2% more damage.; Health Cost (Health): 250; Cooldown: 1s]
The Bouquet of Lies' Petal Barrage ability shoots 3 roses that can travel up to 20 blocks before disappearing. After traveling 5 blocks, it homes in on enemies within 10 blocks. Upon hitting an enemy, it bounces to another enemy up to 4 more times, for a maximum of 5 times. The roses are destroyed upon hitting a block; each flower is a separate entity, continuing to home in on enemies even if another is destroyed, allowing for a maximum of 15 enemies to be hit.
The ability has a cooldown of 1s and consumes 250 [[Health]] on use. This ability cannot be used if the user does not have enough health to be consumed, so using it repeatedly cannot cause fatal damage. Unlike other abilities, the damage does not scale with [[Intelligence]] or [[Ability Damage]]: instead, it uses the same calculation as melee damage, including factoring in [[Strength]] and [[Crit Damage]].
[[Enchantments]] that do not affect abilities, such as [[Sharpness]], [[Life Steal]], [[Drain]], [[Cleave]], and [[Mana Steal]], as well as [[Ferocity]], do not work with the Petal Barrage ability.
## Essence Crafting
Essence Required; [[Wither Essence]]:
|Action|Cost|Cumul. (Cumulative)|
|-|-|-|
|✪|50 Essence|50 Essence|
|✪✪|100 Essence|150 Essence|
|✪✪✪|200 Essence|350 Essence|
|✪✪✪✪|400 Essence; 10,000 [[Coins]]|750 Essence; 10,000 [[Coins]]|
|✪✪✪✪✪|800 Essence; 25,000 [[Coins]]|1,550 Essence; 35,000 [[Coins]]|
## Tips
- The Bouquet of Lies can provide enough [[Strength]] to maximize the [[Crit Damage]] given by the [[Tarantula Armor|Tarantula Helmet]] and the [[Primordial Armor|Primordial Helmet]]'s abilities.
- This weapon pairs better with [[Reforging|Fabled]] due to it having more balanced [[Strength]] and [[Crit Damage]], which will likely result in greater damage infliction.
- Having high [[Vitality]] and [[Health Regen]] can offset this weapon's [[Health]] cost, allowing for its ability to be spammed without a noticeable downside.
## Trivia
- The Bouquet of Lies is frequently abbreviated to BoL.
- It is possible to left and right-click at the same time to deal more [[Damage]] to enemies within melee range.
- Before [[Fel Rose|Fel Roses]] were added to the [[Alpha Hypixel Network]], the Bouquet of Lies required 144 [[Ancient Rose|Ancient Roses]], 128 [[Compost]], a [[Wood Singularity]] and a [[Flower of Truth]], costing a total of to purchase every ingredient from the [[Bazaar]].
- The Petal Barrage ability cannot damage the [[Sven Packmaster]] or [[Ghost|Ghosts]].
- [[One For All]] does not buff the damage of Bouquet of Lies.

Brick Red Dye is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Dyes|Dye]].
## Obtaining
A Brick Red Dye can be obtained from killing any tier of the [[Tarantula Broodfather]]. The drop chance depends on what tier of the slayer boss is killed. This chance is not affected by [[Magic Find]].
|Slayer Boss Tier|Drop Chance|
|-|-|
|Tier I|0.00001% (1 in 10,000,000)|
|Tier II|0.00004% (1 in 2,500,000)|
|Tier III|0.0001% (1 in 1,000,000)|
|Tier IV|0.0002% (1 in 500,000)|
|Tier V|0.0004% (1 in 250,000)|
## Usage
Brick Red Dye can be applied to any vanilla [[Armor]] piece by combining the two items in an [[Anvil]] to change the hex color of that piece to ⁠ #CB4154. It costs 100 [[Bits]] to combine the two items.
It can also be donated to [[Vincent]] to obtain a [[Bucket of Dye]] with a texture unique to its color.

The Bubba Blister is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Power Stones|Power Stone]], which unlocks the [[Power Stones|Bubba]] power for [[Accessories]]. The item is [[Rift Dimension|# Rift-Exportable #]].
## Obtaining
The Bubba Blister is a [[Drop Chance|3Occasional]] drop from the Tier II [[Riftstalker Bloodfiend]] or above if the player has [[Vampire Slayer|Vampire Slayer LVL III (3)]].
Drop Chances:
- Tier II: 15%[Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)]
## Requirements
The Bubba Blister requires [[Combat|Combat XXV (25)]] to be used and can only be used as a Power Stone for [[Accessories]]. Combining 9 Bubba Blisters at [[Maxwell]] will permanently unlock the Bubba [[Power Stones|Power]] for [[Accessories]].

## Tips
- This power is recommended for [[Wolf Slayer]] due to [[Mastiff Armor]] not having any [[Defense]], which is the only downside of this power, and the power granting [[True Defense]] and [[Combat Wisdom]].
- While this power also seems good for [[Blaze Slayer]] due to the [[True Defense]] gain, it is not, as while the [[Inferno Demonlord]] does [[True Damage]], both [[Inferno Demonlord|Quazii]] and [[Inferno Demonlord|Typhoeus]] deal massive amount of regular [[Damage]], which is not reduced by True Defense. However, there are additional ways to get [[Defense]] so it could be a viable, yet risky combination.
- This is the only power that grants bonuses to a [[Wisdom]] stat.

Builder's Ruler
[Effects]
[Ability: Area Planner RIGHT CLICK; Right-Click the top of a block to place a line of blocks. Ability: Organised Builder SNEAK RIGHT CLICK; Sneak Right-Click a block to collect all blocks in a line. Ability: Built-in Storage LEFT CLICK; Opens the ruler storage. Blocks will be placed from your inventory or the ruler storage.]
## Trivia
- The Builder's Ruler is the first item to have three abilities listed.
- The Builder's Ruler has a maximum range of 241 blocks.
- If you consume [[Mycelium]] using a Builder's Ruler, it will turn into dirt.

# Builder's Wand
Aliases: Builder Wand, Builders Wand, Builders wand
Categories: Legendary items, Items, Tools, Other Tools, Bits Shop, Container
[Builder's Wand]
Type: Item
Rarity: LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Enchantable: ✕ No
Reforgeable: ✕ No
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Merchants: [[Elizabeth]]
Buy: 12,000 [[Bits]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: BUILDERS_WAND
Tooltip: Ability: Grand Architect RIGHT CLICK Right-Click the face of a block to extend all connected block faces.; Ability: Built-in Storage LEFT CLICK Opens the wand storage. Blocks will be placed from your inventory or the wand storage.; LEGENDARY
The Builder's Wand is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] item that can significantly speed up building on [[Private Island|Private Islands]] by extending connected block faces. It can be bought from the [[Bits Shop]] for 12,000 [[Bits]].
## Obtaining
The Builder's Wand can be purchased from the [[Bits Shop]] for 12,000 [[Bits]].
## Usage
Item:
Builder's Wand
[Effects]
[Ability: Grand Architect RIGHT CLICK; Right-Click the face of a block to extend all connected block faces. Ability: Built-in Storage LEFT CLICK; Opens the wand storage. Blocks will be placed from your inventory or the wand storage.]
Right-clicking a block to use Ability: Grand Architect will use materials in the inventory or wand storage to extend all connected block faces. The wand can only extend up to 241 blocks, all the same type, at a time. Attempting to extend more gives the error: "You may not Grand Architect that many blocks! (#/241)"
Left-clicking to use the Ability: Built-in Storage will open the wand storage. Blocks in the inventory or wand storage will be placed when using the wand.
With each use of the Builder's Wand, the following message will appear: "You built # blocks! UNDO" Clicking the message will undo the last use of the wand and the following message will appear: "Undid latest Grand Architect use of # blocks!"
Note: Only the last use of the Builder's Wand can be undone. Any previous uses cannot be undone.
### [[Abiphones|Abiphone Contacts]]
A Builder's Wand can be given to [[Builder]] in exchange for their [[Abiphones|Abiphone]] contact.
## Tips
- The [[Builder's Ruler]] is a cheaper alternative that can generally be used as a replacement of this item, although it works slightly differently.
## Trivia
- When paired with the [[InfiniDirt™ Wand]], the Builder's Wand can place [[Dirt]] even when empty. However, each block will cost the user 1 [[Coins|Coin]].
- YouTuber ThirtyVirus originally suggested the Builder's Wand and now owns [[Creative Mind]] #8 because of it.
  - While ThirtyVirus may have suggested it for Hypixel SkyBlock, versions of Builder's Wands have existed in Minecraft for years. Mods and plugins, including Extra Utilities and WorldEdit, have included their own Builder's Wands since 2010.
- The Builder's Wand can be used as a [[Jumbo Backpack Upgrade|Jumbo Backpack]], but it's very cost-ineffective to use it as a substitute for a Jumbo Backpack.
- The Builder's Wand can't place any [[Farm|crops]] and tends to be buggy with some blocks
- The Builder’s Wand is not [[Auction House|auctionable]] with items inside
- The Builder's Wand can be traded with the [[Builder]] for his [[Abiphones|Abiphone]] contact.
## Demonstration
Builder's Wand Demonstration

|Apr 8, 2021|Temporarily disabled the Builder's Wand again.|

|Apr 30, 2021|Fixed an issue where the Builder’s Wand sometimes took too many blocks from your inventory.|
|Apr 30, 2021|Fixed an issue with the Builder’s Wand not working on upper slabs and some stairs.|
|Apr 30, 2021|Fixed an issue where players could use Builder’s Wands as furnace fuel.|
|Apr 30, 2021|Fixed the Builder’s Wand being able to suffocate players.|

The Burning Hedgehog Skin is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Pet Skins|Pet Skin]] that could be purchased from a [[Fire Sale]].
## Obtaining
The Burning Hedgehog Skin could be purchased from the [[Fire Sale]] hosted on January 31, 2026. Being [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]], it cost 950 [[SkyBlock Gems]] and had a limited stock of 3,500 purchases.
## Usage
The Burning Hedgehog Skin can be applied to the player's summoned [[Hedgehog Pet]] by right-clicking it to change its particle trail and appearance. Once applied, left-clicking on the pet as an item will open a menu, allowing for the skin to be changed between 14 different versions.
- This skin will allow you to swap between 14 burning hedgehogs!

## Obtaining
A Byzantium Dye can be obtained from killing any tier of the [[Voidgloom Seraph]]. The drop chance depends on what tier of the slayer boss is killed. This chance is not affected by [[Magic Find]].
|Slayer Boss Tier|Drop Chance|
|-|-|
|Tier I|0.00001% (1 in 10,000,000)|
|Tier II|0.00004% (1 in 2,500,000)|
|Tier III|0.0001% (1 in 1,000,000)|
|Tier IV|0.0002% (1 in 500,000)|
## Usage
Byzantium Dye can be applied to any vanilla [[Armor]] piece by combining the two items in an [[Anvil]] to change the hex color of that piece to ⁠ #702963. It costs 100 [[Bits]] to combine the two items.
It can also be donated to [[Vincent]] to obtain a [[Bucket of Dye]] with a texture unique to its color.

# Celadon Dye
Aliases: Celadon Dye (Old)
Categories: Legendary items, Items, Cosmetics
[Celadon Dye]
Type: Dye ([[Dyes]])
Rarity: LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: DYE_CELADON
Tooltip: Combinable in Anvil; Changes the color of an armor piece to #ACE1AF!; X Rift-Transferable X LEGENDARY DYE
Celadon Dye is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Dyes|Dye]].
## Obtaining
A Celadon Dye has a 0.01% (1 in 10,000) chance of dropping when defeating [[Bacte]]. Additionally, it has a 0.001% (1 in 100,000) chance of dropping when killing a [[Blobbercyst]], which spawns after destroying one of Bacte's tentacles during the boss fight.
## Usage
Celadon Dye can be applied to any vanilla [[Armor]] piece by combining the two items in an [[Anvil]] to change the hex color of that piece to ⁠ #ACE1AF. It costs 100 [[Bits]] to combine the two items.
It can also be donated to [[Vincent]] to obtain a [[Bucket of Dye]] with a texture unique to its color.
## Trivia
- On September 17, 2024, Celadon Dye was a guaranteed drop from defeating [[Bacte]] or [[Blobbercyst]]. This was quickly patched and many of the dyes that were dropped during this time were removed.
## Gallery
- Celadon Dyed Leather Armor
- Celadon Bucket of Dye Celadon [[Bucket of Dye]]

# Celestial Mason Jar
Aliases: Celestial Milk Mixin, Magical Milk Mixin, Mason Jar, Mason Jar (disambiguation), Mason jar
Categories: Legendary items, Items, Craftable items, Mixins
[Celestial Mason Jar]
Type: Mixin
Rarity: LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: CELESTIAL_MASON_JAR
Tooltip: Brewing Ingredient; Mixins provide a buff that can be added to God Potions in a brewing stand and lasts for the full duration.; Grants the following:; - +3 Magic Find - +5 Tracking - +3☯ Global Wisdom - +15 Farming Fortune - +15 Foraging Fortune - +15 Mining Fortune; Duration: 36h 0m; The duration of Mixins can be stacked!; Right-click to consume!; (Requires active Booster Cookie); LEGENDARY
A Celestial Mason Jar is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Mixins|Mixin]] that can be brewed with a [[God Potion]] to grant it an additional effect for its duration.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|1x [[Glass Bottle]] 4x [[Feast Flask]]|Recipe: [[Feast Flask]] ×4 + [[Glass Bottle]] → Celestial Mason Jar|
## Usage
### Brewing
A Celestial Mason Jar can be brewed with a [[God Potion]] to grant the player the following stats:
- +3 [[Magic Find]]
- +5 [[Tracking]]
- +3 [[Wisdom|Global Wisdom]]
- +15 [[Farming Fortune]]
- +15 [[Foraging Fortune]]
- +15 [[Mining Fortune]]
This effect lasts for the [[God Potion]]'s entire duration.
|Ingredients|Brewing ([[Potions]]) Layout|
|-|-|
|[[God Potion]]|Recipe: Celestial Mason Jar → God Potion, God Potion, God Potion|
### Consuming
If the [[Booster Cookie|Cookie Buff]] and [[God Potion]] are both active, Mixins can also be consumed directly by right clicking them. This will provide 3x their normal duration (since a Mixin can be applied to up to 3 [[God Potion|God Potions]]). The timer is separate from the [[God Potion]] timer and is paused if a God Potion is no longer active. The player cannot consume Mixins they do not meet the requirements for.
## Trivia
- The Celestial Mason Jar's name is a reference to 5[[Ranks|[MVP+]]] Celestial_Milk, 7[[Ranks|[YOUTUBE]]] Derailious's partner.
  - The original concept for this item was already created before [[Candidate Aura|Aura]]'s [[Minister Election|Minister Elections]], and then Derailious independently came up with the idea for a [[Tracking]] [[Mixins|Mixin]] (originally to be called Celestial Milk Mixin, and later Magical Milk Mixin). His concept ended up being merged into the [[Harvest Feast]] update and released as the Celestial Mason Jar.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Mar 31, 2026|Added Celestial Mason Jar.|
|Apr 21, 2026|Celestial Mason Jar is now crafted with [[Feast Flask]].|
|Apr 21, 2026|Increased Celestial Mason Jar's duration.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Apr 28, 2026|0.24.4|Added Celestial Mason Jar.|

The Chameleon Shard is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that can be used to create other [[Attributes|Shards]]. Unlike all other shards, it cannot be syphoned into an [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Tree Gifts
A Chameleon Shard can be rarely obtained from [[Tree Gifts]]. It has a 0.08% (1 in 1,250) chance to drop from [[Fig Log|Fig Tree]] and [[Mangrove Log|Mangrove Tree]] gifts, and a 0.1% (1 in 1,000) chance to drop from [[Helix Log|Helix Tree]] gifts.
## Usage
### Fusion
A Chameleon Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]]. Fusing a Chameleon with a Shard grants the next 3 Shards from that Shard's ID line as a result.

# Clover Helmet
Categories: Legendary items, Armor
[Clover Helmet]
Type: [[Armor]]
Rarity: LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Enchantable: ✔ Yes
Reforgeable: ✔ Yes
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: CLOVER_HELMET
Tooltip: Magic Find: +5%; All Other Stats: -5%; Luck is a quantifiable metric that anyone possess in varying amounts.; Sometimes, it is necessary to give up on other skills to improve it.; This item can be reforged!; LEGENDARY HELMET
The Clover Helmet is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Helmets|Helmet]] that increases the player's [[Magic Find]] by 5%, but reduces most other [[Stats]] by 5% (multiplicative). It displays the buyer's name, cost paid, and the details about in which auction it was sold and the ranking of the buyer within that auction. This data disappears if the item is traded in any way.
## Obtaining
### Shen's Auction
The Clover Helmet can sometimes appear on [[Shen's Auction]], and can be bid on by players. The top 10 bidders win the item when the auction ends.
## Usage
Helmet:
Clover Helmet [[Magic Find]]: +5% All Other Stats: -5%
## Tips
- Despite it reducing [[Pet Luck]], the Clover Helmet is still overall a buff to pet drops assuming the player's [[Magic Find]] is higher than their [[Pet Luck]] (without the helmet).
  - This is especially true since [[Pet Luck]] appears to be slowly being phased out and there are no percentage buffs to it anymore.
## Trivia
- The Clover Helmet is the only non-pet item that boosts stats by a percentage on its own.
- The Clover Helmet does not reduce [[Breaking Power]].
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Jul 25, 2022|Added Clover Helmet.|
|Oct 15, 2026|Changed Clover Helmet texture.|
|Oct 15, 2026|Renamed Clover Helmet to Four-leaf Clover.|
|Oct 15, 2026|Reworked Four-leaf Clover from a LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]]) Helmet ([[Helmets]]) to a SPECIAL ([[Rarity]]) Accessory ([[Accessories]]).|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Aug 18, 2022|0.14|Added Clover Helmet.|
|Dec 3, 2024|0.20.9|Clover Helmet no longer reduces [[Breaking Power]].|

### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Cocoaleech Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Groovy Radar]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:

A Condensed Helianthus is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] item. It is the compacted form of [[Helianthus]] and is used to craft [[Helianthus Armor]] and the [[Helianthus Relic]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|9x [[Helianthus]]|Recipe: [[Helianthus]] ×9 → Condensed Helianthus|
## Usage
### NPC Trading
[[Condensed Helianthus]] 5 Condensed Helianthus are needed to purchase a [[Rose Dragon Pet|Rose Dragon Egg]] from [[Ludleth]].
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Helianthus Relic]]|None|1x [[Fermento Artifact]] [[Condensed Helianthus]] 8x Condensed Helianthus|Recipe: Condensed Helianthus ×8 + [[Fermento Artifact]] → [[Helianthus Relic]]|
|Helianthus Helmet ([[Helianthus Armor]])|None|1x Fermento Helmet ([[Fermento Armor]]) [[Condensed Helianthus]] 2x Condensed Helianthus 64x [[Compacted Sunflower]] 64x [[Compacted Wild Rose]] 64x [[Compacted Moonflower]]|Recipe: Condensed Helianthus ×2 + [[Compacted Wild Rose]] x64 + [[Compacted Sunflower]] x64 + [[Fermento Armor]] + [[Compacted Moonflower]] x64 → [[Helianthus Armor]]|
|Helianthus Chestplate ([[Helianthus Armor]])|None|1x Fermento Chestplate ([[Fermento Armor]]) [[Condensed Helianthus]] 2x Condensed Helianthus 64x [[Compacted Sunflower]] 64x [[Compacted Wild Rose]] 64x [[Compacted Moonflower]]|Recipe: Condensed Helianthus ×2 + [[Fermento Armor]] + [[Compacted Sunflower]] x32 ×2 + [[Compacted Wild Rose]] x32 ×2 + [[Compacted Moonflower]] x32 ×2 → [[Helianthus Armor]]|
|Helianthus Leggings ([[Helianthus Armor]])|None|1x Fermento Leggings ([[Fermento Armor]]) [[Condensed Helianthus]] 2x Condensed Helianthus 64x [[Compacted Sunflower]] 64x [[Compacted Wild Rose]] 64x [[Compacted Moonflower]]|Recipe: Condensed Helianthus ×2 + [[Compacted Wild Rose]] x64 + [[Compacted Sunflower]] x32 ×2 + [[Fermento Armor]] + [[Compacted Moonflower]] x32 ×2 → [[Helianthus Armor]]|
|Helianthus Boots ([[Helianthus Armor]])|None|1x Fermento Boots ([[Fermento Armor]]) [[Condensed Helianthus]] 2x Condensed Helianthus 64x [[Compacted Sunflower]] 64x [[Compacted Moonflower]]|Recipe: Condensed Helianthus ×2 + [[Compacted Sunflower]] x64 + [[Fermento Armor]] + [[Compacted Moonflower]] x64 → [[Helianthus Armor]]|
### Composter
Condensed Helianthus can be used to fill up the [[Composter]]'s Crop Meter by 270k Organic Matter.

# Copper Dye
Categories: Legendary items, Items, Cosmetics
[Copper Dye]
Type: Dye ([[Dyes]])
Rarity: LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: DYE_COPPER
Tooltip: Combinable in Anvil; Changes the color of an armor piece to #B87333!; LEGENDARY DYE
Copper Dye is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Dyes|Dye]].
## Obtaining
Copper Dye can be obtained by completing offers for [[The Garden|Visitors]] on [[The Garden]]. The drop chance depends on the [[Rarity]] of the Visitor.
|Visitor Rarity|Drop Chance|
|-|-|
|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|0.001% (1 in 100,000)|
|RARE ([[Rarity]])|0.002% (1 in 50,000)|
|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|0.004% (1 in 25,000)|
|MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|0.02% (1 in 5,000)|
|SPECIAL ([[Rarity]])|0.2% (1 in 500)|
## Usage
Copper Dye can be applied to any [[Armor]] piece by combining the two items in an [[Anvil]] to change the hex color of that piece to ⁠ #B87333. It costs 100 [[Bits]] to combine the items.
It can also be donated to [[Vincent]] to obtain a [[Bucket of Dye]] with a texture unique to its color.

- Copper Bucket of Dye Copper [[Bucket of Dye]]
